### 暮色雨林中的狼人变身与巨兽决斗

> Enter the jungle. Unleash the beast. Witness the battle. FANGS OF THE EMERALD DUSK, made with Seedance 2.5 on @WizstarAI Create your next cinematic masterpiece …

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/1db67b16bf03.jpg" width="600" alt="暮色雨林中的狼人变身与巨兽决斗">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-create-an-exactly-30-second-16-9-photorealistic-hollywood-creature-action-film-f3d3687d73c5?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Xaroon_x | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/Xaroon_x/status/2116895635114717388) | **发布:** 2026-11-01 | **热度:** 69

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（24 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
FANGS OF THE EMERALD DUSK

[ENGINE DIRECTIVE]
Create an exactly 30-second, 16:9 photorealistic Hollywood creature-action film. 60fps where supported, realistic textures, cinematic lighting, volumetric mist and natural motion blur.

CHARACTER LOCK: @image1 is the human hero. Preserve his exact face, skin tone, hair, beard, body, black outfit and props. @image2 is his werewolf form; match its fur, muzzle, eyes, ears, claws and proportions exactly in every shot. No human features or clothing remain in werewolf form. One consistent creature, no identity drift. Antagonist: a 4-meter muscular jungle behemoth with moss-covered bark armor, cracked antler-like skull crest, curved tusks and massive arms.

LOCATION: Rain-soaked ancient jungle clearing at dusk, giant buttress-root trees, hanging vines, fallen mossy log on the left, rocky muddy center and golden light shafts through mist. Fixed geography; hero enters from left, behemoth from right. Broken trees and environmental damage persist.

[30-SECOND TIMELINE]

00–05s | STANDOFF: Wide establishing shot, hero left and behemoth right, 30 meters apart. Boot and eye close-ups, beast eye close-up, chest heaving. They roar and sprint toward each other, splashing mud.

05–10s | TRANSFORMATION: Fast tracking shot. Both leap. In dramatic slow motion, the hero transforms completely into @image2: bones shift, muscles expand, fur erupts, face forms a wolf muzzle, claws extend, clothes vanish. They collide mid-air, blasting mist and leaves outward, then crash into mud.

10–15s | BRAWL: Werewolf claws the beast's shoulder and dodges its swing. The beast backhands him into a tree, cracking the trunk. The werewolf rebounds, lunges with both claws, and the beast slams the ground, spraying mud.

15–20s | REVERSAL: The beast pins the werewolf and thrusts its tusks downward. Werewolf catches its jaw, braces against its chest and kicks it into the fallen log. He rises and roars.

20–25s | FINAL COMBO: The beast charges. Werewolf runs up the broken leaning trunk, leaps over it, slashes its back mid-spin, then lands a knee strike and spinning kick. The beast staggers, knees buckling.

25–30s | FINISH: Slow motion: werewolf leaps and smashes both fists onto the beast's skull, driving it into cracked mud. The beast collapses motionless. Werewolf breathes heavily; in close-up, fur recedes, muzzle shortens, and he becomes exactly @image1 with original outfit restored. He smiles calmly and walks into golden mist. Hard cut at 30 seconds.

AUDIO: Original tribal orchestral score, war drums, low strings, taiko percussion, choir swell during transformation, synchronized bone cracks, fabric tearing, claws, roars, splintering wood and heavy impacts. Brief silence before final blow, thunderous kill impact, then warm strings and rain fading at exactly 30 seconds.
```

</details>

### 赤红能量战甲英雄雨夜激战蒙面人

> Pushing AI filmmaking to the next level with cinematic visuals, seamless transformations, and action-packed storytelling. #Loovaai Powered by Seedance 2.5 on @L…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/0dc41c20a4a2.jpg" width="600" alt="赤红能量战甲英雄雨夜激战蒙面人">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-crimson-flashpoint-48-seconds-16-9-12-shots-4-seconds-eaef000addde?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Xaroon_x | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/Xaroon_x/status/2110248837432439129) | **发布:** 2026-10-14 | **热度:** 69

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（37 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
CRIMSON FLASHPOINT — 48 seconds, 16:9, 12 shots × 4 seconds. Photorealistic Hollywood superhero action film, ARRI ALEXA 65, anamorphic look, subtle film grain.

LOCATION: Rain-soaked neon downtown intersection at night, glass skyscrapers, abstract cyan/magenta/amber signs, reflective wet asphalt, steam grates, parked cars, dark alley on the right. Heavy rain throughout; crimson is the dominant accent.

HERO (@image1): Preserve identical face, hair, beard, skin tone, age, and build in every shot. Original outfit before transformation; afterward, matte-black articulated armor with glowing crimson seams across ribs, arms, legs, and spine, segmented chest plate with a faint red core, fingerless armored gloves. No helmet or cape; face fully visible.

ENEMIES: Exactly three masked men in black tactical jackets, cargo pants, grey gloves, and matte-black full-face masks. Man 1 is tall and lean with a steel baton; Man 2 is broad with a crowbar; Man 3 has a medium build and baton. No dialogue.

SHOT 01 (00–04): Extreme-wide low-angle skyline under storm clouds. A bright crimson streak cuts diagonally through the sky, illuminating rain and glass towers.

SHOT 02 (04–08): Fast overhead tracking follows the falling streak toward street level. A faint human silhouette appears inside the light as crimson reflections sweep across the road.

SHOT 03 (08–12): Hero lands in a one-knee, one-fist crouch in his original outfit. Water explodes outward; red energy threads crawl across his arms and torso.

SHOT 04 (12–16): Smooth 360° orbit as crimson energy assembles the black armor panel by panel. The chest plate locks into place and red seams ignite.

SHOT 05 (16–20): 85mm close-up. Rain beads on his face and armor; a red spark flickers in his eyes as he looks forward with controlled determination.

SHOT 06 (20–24): Low side-tracking shot. Fully armored hero sprints down the wet street at superhuman speed, spraying water and leaving a thin crimson trail.

SHOT 07 (24–28): Over-the-shoulder shot. Three masked men emerge from the right-side alley, spreading out with weapons ready. Hero skids to a defensive stop.

SHOT 08 (28–32): Hero dashes forward as a crimson blur and strikes Man 1, launching him backward. Rain and water burst outward in slow motion.

SHOT 09 (32–36): Man 2 swings his crowbar. Hero ducks, spins, and lands one strike, sending him sliding through a puddle toward a parked car.

SHOT 10 (36–40): Man 3 lunges with his baton. Hero dodges inches away and delivers a powerful palm strike, blasting him backward through an explosive ring of water. Bright crimson flash at impact.

SHOT 11 (40–44): Wide low-angle pull-back. Hero stands calmly between all three fallen attackers as rain pours over his armor and crimson seams gradually dim.

SHOT 12 (44–48): Extreme close-up of the hero lifting his head with quiet confidence. A tiny red spark crosses his eyes before the image cuts to black.

AUDIO: Original cinematic orchestral-synth score, heavy rain, electrical crackle, realistic impacts, water splashes, accelerating percussion, and a powerful musical peak at Shot 10. No dialogue.

CONTINUITY: Maintain exact hero identity, armor design, enemy count, location layout, lighting, and screen direction across all shots. Realistic physics, consistent rain, natural motion blur, controlled speed ramps, clean cuts, and stable camera movement.

NEGATIVE: Cartoon, artificial CGI appearance, plastic skin, face changes, inconsistent armor, extra characters, duplicate bodies, extra fingers, deformed hands, cape, helmet, yellow accents, lightning-bolt emblem, blood, gore, impossible physics, random camera movement, jitter, flicker, readable text, logos, or watermark.
```

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（43 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Shot 1 (0–1.2s): Character A, matching reference face/outfit, sits beside a passenger on a dim train, feverish, sweating, staring strangely at him.

Shot 2 (1.2–2.4s): Close-up: her trembling hand, faint dark veins appearing, shallow unnatural breathing.

Shot 3 (2.4–3.6s): Her head tilts unnaturally toward him, glassy unfocused eyes, tense profile close-up.

Shot 4 (3.6–4.8s): She suddenly lunges and bites his forearm, teeth breaking skin, his face frozen in shock.

Shot 5 (4.8–6s): Close-up of the wound as dark veins rapidly branch outward, skin blackening.

Shot 6 (6–7.2s): Veins race toward his elbow; skin pales, sweat beads on his forehead.

Shot 7 (7.2–8.5s): His eyes cloud milky white, pupils dilate, breathing becomes wet and ragged.

Shot 8 (8.5–9.7s): His body stiffens and twitches; black veins spread across his neck and jaw.

Shot 9 (9.7–11s): He throws his head back and screams inhumanly, face covered in black veins, slow motion.

Shot 10 (11–12.2s): His head snaps forward; bloodshot empty eyes and slightly unhinged jaw reveal the completed transformation.

Shot 11 (12.2–13.5s): He instantly lunges at another passenger and bites them, sudden violent impact.

Shot 12 (13.5–14.8s): Wider shot: nearby passengers turn, confusion becoming horror.

Shot 13 (14.8–16s): The bitten passenger stumbles back, clutching the wound as dark veins spread up the arm.

Shot 14 (16–17.3s): Passengers rise in panic, grabbing bags and backing away, wide cinematic shot.

Shot 15 (17.3–18.5s): The new victim convulses violently in the aisle as passengers recoil; overhead lights flicker.

Shot 16 (18.5–19.7s): Eyes roll back, then open bloodshot and empty; jaw twitches—the second transformation complete.

Shot 17 (19.7–21s): He lunges at the nearest passenger; screams erupt as the car descends into chaos, handheld camera.

Shot 18 (21–22.5s): Passengers scramble over seats and luggage toward the exit, bags spilling everywhere.

Shot 19 (22.5–24s): A group rushes toward the connecting door, looking back as infection spreads behind them.

Shot 20 (24–25.5s): Door slams shut; survivors barricade it with luggage and cushions as infected hands pound from behind.

Shot 21 (25.5–27.5s): Close-up through the glass: infected faces press against it as cracks spread, emergency lights pulsing.

Shot 22 (27.5–30s): Survivors huddle against the far wall, breathing hard and staring at the shaking door as train lights streak outside, cinematic outbreak ending.
```

</details>

```
Subject
A miniature hand-drawn 2D animated girl with curly black hair, a yellow apron, red sneakers, and a tiny wooden spoon, cooking inside a realistic full-sized kitchen.

Style
Mixed-reality hybrid animation combining playful 2D hand-drawn doodle character art with a photorealistic live-action kitchen. Whimsical stop-motion physics, expressive line animation, exaggerated scale, warm cinematic lighting, and playful cooking sound effects.

Camera & Framing
Macro close-ups, low-angle tracking shots, overhead kitchen perspectives, dramatic push-ins, and shallow depth of field emphasizing the tiny chef against oversized kitchen objects.

Audio & Scene Breakdown

Visual: The tiny chef stands beside a giant mixing bowl, struggling to climb onto a wooden spoon before using it as a makeshift ladder.

Visual: She jumps into the bowl and rapidly mixes pancake batter while the whisk spins around her like a giant amusement-park ride.

Visual: She slides down a stream of pancake batter onto a massive frying pan, narrowly avoiding a falling blueberry.

Visual: The pancake suddenly flips into the air. She jumps onto it and rides it like a surfboard as it spins above the pan.

Visual: She lands on a giant plate, then uses a strawberry as a trampoline to bounce onto a stack of pancakes.

Visual: She proudly places a tiny strawberry on top, but the strawberry rolls away. She chases it across the enormous plate.

Visual: The camera pulls back to reveal the finished pancake breakfast. The tiny chef sits on the edge holding her spoon proudly—then a giant fork suddenly enters frame and lifts the entire pancake stack away. She freezes in disbelief.

Audio: Playful kitchen ambience, whisking, batter splashes, pancake flip, tiny footsteps, comedic impact sounds, subtle upbeat music, no dialogue.
```

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（6 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Sometimes the smallest moments become the most magical adventures. 🌧️🚂✨

Created on seedance 2.5

Prompt
Create a 15-second cinematic fantasy slice-of-life sequence following a young boy waiting alone at a quiet rural railway station on a rainy evening. Begin with a wide establishing shot across multiple wet railway tracks, slowly revealing a small countryside station surrounded by endless green fields, distant mountains, utility poles, and a dark cloudy sky → reveal the young boy standing beneath the station shelter, wearing a bright yellow raincoat, white shorts, and vivid red rain boots, quietly waiting for his train as heavy rain falls around him → transition to a low-angle cinematic close-up of his red rain boots standing beside a shallow rain puddle, with the warm reflection of a small station lantern shimmering across the wet ground → cut to a side profile shot of the boy sitting alone on a wooden railway bench, looking quietly toward the empty tracks while rain falls in the background → transition to a mysterious close-up beneath the wooden bench as a tiny strange green creature wearing an oversized pointed green hat slowly peeks out from the shadows, watching the boy with large curious eyes → cut to a cinematic close-up of the boy's face as he notices the mysterious creature, his expression changing naturally from surprise to gentle curiosity and a warm smile → transition to a detailed close-up of the boy opening a small cloth lunch bag and taking out a piece of bread while sitting on the bench → cut to the tiny green creature watching the food from beneath the bench, cautiously moving closer with innocent curiosity → transition to a medium shot as the boy gently places a piece of bread on the bench for the little creature, creating a quiet emotional connection between them → cut to a wide cinematic shot of the empty railway tracks stretching into the rainy countryside as a distant train headlight slowly appears on the horizon → transition into a dramatic forward tracking shot along the wet railway tracks, the approaching train becoming brighter as rain continues falling and its warm headlights reflect across the rails → cut back to the station as the train arrives and stops beside the platform, its warm interior lights glowing against the cold blue rainy atmosphere → final shot becomes a cinematic medium-wide shot of the young boy stepping into the warmly illuminated train while briefly looking back toward the tiny green creature near the bench, as rain continues falling outside and the train prepares to leave the quiet countryside station
```

</details>

Made with seedance 2.5

Prompt 👇 
Create a cute, heartwarming, ultra-realistic 3D animated video of an adorable fluffy cream-colored baby bunny with huge glossy brown eyes, long soft ears, rosy cheeks, tiny pink nose, and delicate eyelashes.

The bunny wears a soft pink gingham headband with long bunny ears and a matching pink-and-white checkered scarf tied into a cute bow around its neck.

The scene is bright, cheerful, and whimsical, set outdoors beneath a vivid blue sky filled with soft fluffy white clouds. Use a close-up portrait composition with the bunny centered in the frame, looking directly into the camera.

The bunny makes natural, playful facial expressions throughout the video: gently tilting its head from side to side, blinking, winking, opening its mouth in a cute surprised expression, smiling brightly, and giggling happily with its eyes partially closed. Toward the end, the bunny raises its tiny paws near its chest and gives an irresistibly adorable happy expression.

Use subtle, smooth character animation with realistic fur movement, expressive eyes, soft facial motion, and natural ear movement. Keep the character consistent throughout the entire video.

Style: high-end cinematic 3D animation, ultra-detailed soft fur, adorable Disney/Pixar-inspired charm, realistic lighting, soft depth of field, vibrant colors, polished textures, cute fantasy atmosphere.

Camera: static close-up portrait shot, slight natural camera movement, centered composition, vertical 9:16 format.

Lighting: bright natural daylight, soft warm highlights, gentle shadows, clean and vibrant outdoor illumination.

Mood: joyful, innocent, playful, wholesome, cute, and heartwarming.

Duration: approximately 10 seconds.

Avoid text, subtitles, logos, watermarks, distorted facial features, extra limbs, unnatural movements, flickering, or changes in character appearance.
```

</details>

### 亡母来电阻止雨夜山路车祸

> Created with Seedance 2.5 A cinematic thriller about a mysterious warning, a dangerous turn, and a call that may have saved her life. Prompt: Create a 30-second…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/2dbd04dccd94.jpg" width="600" alt="亡母来电阻止雨夜山路车祸">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-create-a-30-second-ultra-photorealistic-hollywood-level-cinematic-thriller-scen-364b9e454242?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @RuzainaMeer | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/RuzainaMeer/status/2097193955230405007) | **发布:** 2026-09-08 | **热度:** 69

**稳定度：** 84/100

**复测：** Grok Imagine · 2026-10-03 · ✅ 复现 (84.4 分) · [产物](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-create-a-30-second-ultra-photorealistic-hollywood-level-cinematic-thriller-scen-364b9e454242/video-grok-imagine-20261003-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（80 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second ultra-photorealistic Hollywood-level cinematic thriller scene titled “THE LAST CALL.” The entire sequence must feel like a scene from a high-budget live-action feature film, with realistic human acting, natural facial expressions, physically accurate movement, realistic rain, practical lighting, believable vehicle physics, detailed environments, cinematic depth of field, subtle film grain, natural skin texture, realistic reflections, and professional Hollywood cinematography. No anime, no CGI-looking visuals, no fantasy aesthetic, no cyberpunk, no neon lighting, no exaggerated visual effects.
MAIN CHARACTER: A realistic young woman in her late 20s, natural appearance, expressive eyes, shoulder-length dark hair slightly damp from the humid weather, wearing a simple dark jacket. Maintain the exact same woman, face, hairstyle, clothing, car, weather and environment consistently throughout the entire sequence.

SETTING: A remote mountain road at night during heavy but realistic rainfall. Dark forested mountains surround the narrow winding road. Wet asphalt reflects only natural headlights and road markings. The atmosphere is tense, isolated and believable.

0–5 SECONDS

Interior of a moving car at night. Medium shot from the passenger side showing the woman driving alone. Rain continuously hits the windshield and realistic windshield wipers move back and forth. Dashboard illumination softly lights her face. She looks tired and emotionally distant while concentrating on the road. Camera slowly pushes toward her face. Natural breathing, subtle blinking and realistic micro-expressions.

5–10 SECONDS

Her smartphone suddenly begins ringing in the center console. Cut to an extremely realistic close-up of the phone screen.

Caller ID: “MOM”

The woman freezes.

Her eyes widen slightly. Her hands tighten around the steering wheel. She looks at the phone in disbelief. She hesitates for a moment, then reaches down and answers the call.

10–17 SECONDS

Extreme close-up of her face as she listens.

A faint, emotional female voice comes through the phone:

“Don't take the next turn.”

The woman's expression changes from confusion to fear.

The call suddenly disconnects.

She looks at the phone.

The screen reads: CALL ENDED

Outside the windshield, a sharp mountain turn approaches through the heavy rain.

17–23 SECONDS

She immediately slows the car.

Camera moves to a forward-facing perspective through the windshield. The road curves sharply ahead.

Suddenly, distant headlights appear through the rain around the corner.

The woman looks toward them.

A large truck suddenly loses control and crashes through the roadside barrier at the exact location where her car would have been if she had continued driving.

The crash must look physically realistic and grounded, with believable vehicle momentum, rain, debris and impact. No exaggerated explosions.

23–27 SECONDS

Silence after the crash.

The woman sits completely still, breathing heavily, visibly shaken.

She slowly looks down at her phone.

The phone screen now shows:

NO SIM — NO SIGNAL

She looks confused and terrified.

27–30 SECONDS

Extreme close-up of her eyes filling with tears.

She looks toward the empty passenger seat.

A subtle emotional pause.

In a trembling whisper, she says:

“Mom?”

Cut instantly to black.

FINAL AUDIO: Heavy rain, windshield wipers, realistic car interior ambience, distant thunder, phone ringing, muffled female voice, sudden silence after the crash, then only the woman's breathing.
```

Create a 30-second, 16:9 premium live-action vampire-romance teaser for an international short-drama audience. Use Image 1 for the two leads' appearance and Image 2 for the castle environment. Preserve both identities consistently throughout every shot with ultra-realistic live-action cinematography, cinematic lighting, natural skin texture, and emotionally expressive performances.

Story: A curious young woman accidentally wanders into the forbidden wing of a forgotten castle, where she awakens an ancient vampire noble from centuries of sleep. The instant he sees her, he senses the bloodline of an ancient war flowing through her veins. Instead of killing her, he becomes dangerously fascinated. His obsession grows immediately into a possessive desire to keep her close. She is frightened by his overwhelming presence but refuses to surrender completely, meeting his dominance with quiet courage.

Style: Premium ReelShort / DramaBox vampire romance. Darkly romantic, emotionally intense, fate-bound, mysterious, elegant, and seductive without becoming explicit. Build escalating romantic tension through eye contact, silence between lines, restrained emotion, and powerful chemistry. The pacing should feel like the opening episode of a viral international drama with a shocking cliffhanger ending. No gore, no cheap horror, no Halloween costumes, no monsters, and no modern streetwear.

The castle is illuminated only by moonlight filtering through towering stained-glass windows, flickering candlelight, fireplaces, drifting mist, ancient chandeliers, marble floors, velvet curtains, towering bookshelves, gothic arches, carved stone walls, and floating dust particles. Rich black, crimson, silver, and deep blue dominate the color palette.

Shoot entirely for TikTok, ReelShort, and DramaBox. Prioritize medium close-ups, close-ups, extreme close-ups, slow push-ins, over-the-shoulder shots, dramatic eye contact, trembling hands, subtle breathing, and intimate framing. Every shot should maximize emotional tension between the leads.

Scene 1 (0–5s)
The young woman cautiously walks through the abandoned castle corridor holding an antique candle. The flame flickers violently as if something unseen is watching her.

Woman (whispering):
"I shouldn't be here..."

A distant male voice echoes through the darkness.

Vampire (O.S., whisper):
"...You finally came."

Scene 2 (5–10s)
An enormous stone coffin slowly opens. The vampire noble rises with quiet elegance, his crimson eyes fixed only on her. She freezes in fear.

Woman:
"Who... who are you?"

Vampire (calmly):
"The one fate refused to bury."

Scene 3 (10–15s)
He slowly approaches until only inches separate them. He gently lifts her chin without hurting her, studying her face as though recognizing someone long forgotten.

Vampire:
"Your blood remembers a war the world has forgotten."

Woman:
"I don't know what you're talking about."

Scene 4 (15–20s)
She steps backward, refusing to surrender despite her fear. He blocks her escape with effortless confidence.

Woman:
"I'm not afraid of you."

Vampire (soft smile):
"You should be."

Scene 5 (20–25s)
He leans closer, eyes locked with hers. Their breathing becomes the only sound. His expression shifts from curiosity to obsession.

Vampire:
"I searched centuries for you..."

Woman (barely whispering):
"Why me?"

Vampire:
"Because you belong to my destiny."

Scene 6 (25–30s)
The castle doors slam shut by themselves. Candle flames extinguish one by one. He gently reaches for her hand while she hesitates, torn between fear and an irresistible pull.

Vampire:
"You may fight fate..."

(He slowly intertwines his fingers with hers.)

Vampire:
"...but you'll never escape me."

Final shot: Extreme close-up of their locked eyes as orchestral music swells. Smash cut to black before they kiss.

Editing should feel premium and fast-paced with cinematic hard cuts, dramatic push-ins, elegant slow motion, realistic sound design, orchestral strings, deep piano notes, distant thunder, candle crackles, footsteps, soft breathing, fabric movement, and subtle heartbeats. The final frame ends on a cliffhanger that leaves the audience desperate for Episode 2.

### 冬夜沙发上的抢毯大战

> The winter is cold, but their little love war is HOT! 🥶🔥 Sometimes romance is just fighting over a blanket… and winning a kiss. 💋😌 🎥 Made with @GlbGPT #GLO…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/8c1fe2ed79c4.jpg" width="600" alt="冬夜沙发上的抢毯大战">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-seedance-2-5-16-9-30s-blanket-war-korean-romantic-comedy-shor-fb00f375aa67?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @abulu8 | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/abulu8/status/2124523191515414643) | **发布:** 2026-11-22 | **热度:** 68

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（54 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
SEEDANCE 2.5 — 16:9 — 30s — "이불 전쟁 (Blanket War)" — Korean romantic-comedy short, multi-shot cinematic, Korean dialogue, natural ASMR only (no music, no BGM).

REFERENCES
Image1 — location (cozy Seoul rooftop room / 옥탑방, winter night, about 10pm: a snowy-city attic window at LEFT with thin cream curtains pulled aside and faint falling snow outside; a paper-shade floor lamp, already on, just left of the sofa; a compact white heater glowing orange on the floor at LEFT under the window; a small dark-walnut wedding frame on the back wall, faces unreadable; a cream mini-fridge and kettle out of focus behind the sofa to the left; ONE compact oatmeal bouclé two-seat sofa in the CENTER, facing camera; the brick-red / cream / mustard plaid sherpa throw draped on the RIGHT seat and backrest, one corner hanging down; a low walnut table in front of the sofa with a cream cocoa mug at LEFT, a sage-green cocoa mug at RIGHT, a mint bowl of chips, a small plate of pear wedges, a black remote and a half-eaten dark-chocolate bar; a wooden cabinet with a plant and a basket at RIGHT. The TV is off-camera in FRONT of the sofa and is never seen; only its cool-blue flicker glow lands on the sofa and on faces).
Image2 — character sheet (Oh Seonwoo, 28, husband: tousled dark-chocolate brown bed-head with a left-swept side part; an oversized washed-sage zip hoodie worn open over a heather-navy waffle-knit henley; charcoal-grey lounge joggers; thick cream crew socks, no shoes; a plain slim polished yellow-gold wedding band, 2.5 mm wide, no stones and no engraving, on his LEFT ring finger only).
Image3 — character sheet (Jung Yena, 26, wife: cool black hair in a slightly messy mid-high bun, no bangs; a chunky matte coral-orange claw clip at the back-right of the head; an oversized butter-yellow chunky knit sweater over a cream tee and cream pajama shorts; thick dusty-rose fuzzy socks, no shoes; a matching plain slim polished yellow-gold wedding band, 2.5 mm wide, no stones and no engraving, on her LEFT ring finger only).

PROP LOCKS
- PROP-BLANKET: exactly ONE blanket for the entire clip — the thick sherpa throw from Image1, brick-red / cream / mustard tartan windowpane check on the outer face, plain cream sherpa on the reverse, 8 cm solid brick-red binding on all edges. At 0s it is already pulled off the backrest and spread loosely across BOTH their laps (as in Image1's design, now in use), and both hold an edge. After she wins the tug it is wrapped fully around her; she later opens one side for him, then throws the same blanket over both of their heads, then pulls it down off her own head, and it stays around both of them on the sofa to the end. FIXED SIZE: a large throw about 150 × 200 cm, the SAME size and the SAME tartan check scale in every shot — big enough from the start to wrap her fully and to cover both of their heads; it never shrinks, never grows, never stretches to drape the whole sofa. The sofa itself is bare oatmeal bouclé: NO grey blanket, NO second throw, NO folded blanket on the backrest, NO cushion cover that looks like a blanket. When she yanks it, the WHOLE blanket leaves his hands and goes to her — no leftover piece, no duplicate, no phantom copy staying on his seat. Never a second blanket, never a color change, never a different pattern, never swapped for a plain duvet.
- PROP-TABLE: the walnut table and everything on it stay exactly as in Image1 and are never touched: cream mug at LEFT, sage-green mug at RIGHT, mint chips bowl, pear plate, black remote, half-eaten chocolate. Nobody drinks, nobody eats, nothing is knocked over or moved.
- PROP-FRAME: the wedding frame on the back wall is a SMALL 15 × 20 cm dark-walnut frame holding a LIGHT, bright wedding photo (white dress, floral arch). It stays exactly where it is in Image1, at the same size, in every shot. It never moves, never enlarges, never turns black, and never becomes a TV, monitor or dark rectangle.
- PROP-RINGS: a plain slim polished yellow-gold wedding band, 2.5 mm, on Image2's LEFT RING FINGER (the fourth finger) only, never on the index finger, sitting cleanly on the skin, never warped or melting; a matching identical band on Image3's LEFT ring finger only. No rings on either right hand. No silver rings. No extra rings. No stones or engraving.
- HAND ANATOMY: every hand is anatomically correct, with five fingers, the thumb on the correct side, and the right hand on the right arm and the left hand on the left arm. No morphing or fused fingers during the tug, the lip wipe or the face-cover.
- Wardrobe and accessories exactly as in the refs: his sage hoodie stays open; her butter-yellow knit stays on; her hair accessory is ONE chunky matte coral-orange plastic CLAW CLIP (hard, toothed, about 9 cm wide, the same size and shape as in Image3) fixed at the back-right of the bun in EVERY shot, close-ups included — never a scrunchie, never fuzzy, never red, never smaller, never sliding to the side of her head — even after the blanket comes off and her hair is slightly messed; both remain in socks, no shoes.

STAGING LOCK
- Image2 is already seated on the sofa's LEFT seat, Image3 already seated on the sofa's RIGHT seat. From the main camera they are frame-LEFT and frame-RIGHT. Both stay on those seats for the entire clip, generally facing the camera / the off-camera TV in front of the sofa. No seat swap, no standing, no walking, no leaving the sofa, no shoulder lean, no sitting on each other's lap.
- When she wins the blanket she may twist her upper body slightly toward screen-right, still seated on the RIGHT seat; she turns back to face the TV when she lets him in. When he slides under the opened blanket he stays on the LEFT seat and only shifts closer along the sofa, still on his side.
- Camera axis: the main camera sits in front of the sofa, roughly where the unseen TV is, looking toward the couple, the window at frame-LEFT and the wooden cabinet at frame-RIGHT. Keep coverage simple and smooth: the wide two-shot from the front plus clean close-ups, with every cut matching posture, blanket position and hand placement exactly so nothing jumps. The camera NEVER goes under the blanket; every blanket moment is seen from outside, in front of the sofa. Never cross the line, and the TV / any screen must never enter the frame. Each speaker is clearly on screen and in focus when they speak.

ENVIRONMENT LOCK
The room is quietly alive, but there are no other people anywhere. Lock only this background life, consistent across shots:
- LEFT WINDOW: a light, slow winter snow falling outside, city lights soft and still, faint condensation at the bottom of the glass.
- LEFT FLOOR: the compact heater stays in place, coils glowing a quiet orange.
- LAMP: the paper floor lamp stays on, unmoving, just left of the sofa.
- TABLE: a very faint thread of steam may rise from the two untouched cocoa mugs.
- TV GLOW: a soft cool-blue flicker, changing gently, falls on their faces and the front of the sofa only. The set itself is never seen.
- BACKGROUND: the mini-fridge, kettle, wedding frame and the RIGHT cabinet with plant and basket stay where they are in Image1. No extra people, no pets, no hallway figures.

LOOK / LIGHTING LOCK (identical in every shot)
Winter-night 옥탑방, about 10pm, exactly as in Image1: a warm amber key from the paper floor lamp at the left of the sofa, a cool-blue TV-glow fill from off-camera in front of them, and a soft cool snow-city light from the LEFT window; the heater a small orange practical at floor-left. This single mixed lighting stays the same in every shot, INCLUDING EVERY CLOSE-UP: faces in close-up keep the same warm-amber lamp key on the side of the face nearest the lamp, with only a soft blue TV kiss on top. Close-ups are NEVER cooler, flatter, bluer or more neutral than the wide shots; it does NOT shift to daylight, does not warm into morning, does not lose the TV flicker, and no color grade changes between shots. Low saturation, intimate, filmic, cozy and a little tight. Skin stays luminous; no harsh orange-teal split, no beauty-key glamour lighting.

SOUND
Natural ASMR only, throughout: a faint muffled TV movie track with no clear words, no recognizable song and no audible dialogue from the set; the sherpa-fleece blanket rustling and sliding; the fake sneeze; a low heater hum; a soft snow-wind at the window; the short cheek-kiss sound; a muffled giggle and breath from under the blanket; sofa-cushion shifts; breaths. No passerby voices, no neighbors, no phone, no music. NO laugh track, NO studio audience, NO background chatter or crowd murmur. The only laughter is one short muffled giggle from her under the blanket.
Music: NONE at any time. No BGM, no score, no instruments of any kind.
Voices: ONLY these two lines, spoken by the character clearly on screen and in focus at that moment. Image2: a clearly MALE voice, low-to-mid baritone, slightly husky, soft and a little pitiful in tone but never high-pitched, never feminine, never childlike. His 「…추워.」 must sound unmistakably like a grown man, lips moving in exact sync. Image3: a cute, grumpy young adult female voice, reluctant on her line. The male and female voices never merge and never swap. No other voices: no narrator, no TV dialogue you can make out, no third person. A muffled giggle under the blanket is a laugh only, not a spoken line.

PACING
Playful and warm, with short silent pauses after the tug, after the sneeze, after the cheek kiss, and under the blanket. Let the smug wrap, the fake-innocent face, the cute grumpy glare, his subtle stunned freeze, his smirk back and her shy face-cover land. No music. No fast-forward feel, no frantic cutting.

BEATS
0–5s — REAL TUG-OF-WAR. Establishing two-shot from in front of the sofa, the snowy window at frame-left, the paper lamp glowing, the off-camera TV's cool-blue flicker on their faces: Image2 on the LEFT seat, Image3 on the RIGHT, the ONE plaid sherpa throw spread across both laps, each gripping an edge with both hands. This is a genuine back-and-forth struggle, NOT one single pull: (1) he tugs hard and the blanket slides toward him, uncovering her knees; (2) she leans back and tugs it back, it slides to her side; (3) he yanks again, both lean away from each other, the blanket stretched TAUT between them, both laughing through clenched teeth; (4) she gives one final strong yank and it rips free from his hands. Both stay seated the whole time. She wraps the whole blanket fully around herself, smug, and twists her upper body slightly toward screen-right, still on the RIGHT seat. He is left with empty hands. Fleece rustle, sofa creak.
5–9s — Close-up of Image2, clearly on screen: he looks cold and pitiful, shoulders drawing in, then fakes a sneeze that is obviously fake but REALISTIC and restrained: a small inhale, a short "e-chwi", his head dipping once. It is not wild or theatrical, with no huge wind-up and no flailing. Right after it, mouth in sync, a little pitiful:
   Image2: 「…추워.」
   He looks at her with hopeful puppy eyes. A short silent beat.
9–13s — Close-up of Image3, still wrapped, she sighs and rolls her eyes, then turns back toward him. Mouth in sync, reluctant, cute-grumpy:
   Image3: 「…들어와.」
   With her LEFT arm (the side nearest him) she lifts open the LEFT edge of the same blanket, the cream sherpa reverse visible for a moment. He slides along the sofa, still on the LEFT seat, and slips in close under it. Fleece rustle.
13–17s — Two-shot: while she looks toward the TV, he leans in and sneaks a quick kiss on her LEFT cheek (the cheek nearest him). He instantly faces the TV again, pretending innocence: lips pressed, eyebrows up, eyes wide. She turns her head slowly toward him with a cute grumpy glare that is not really angry, cheeks puffed, eyes narrowed. A silent pause.
17–22s — FORCED KISS UNDER THE BLANKET, WITH CLEAR MOVEMENT. She suddenly grabs the same blanket and throws it up over both their heads. Hold on the covered shape on the sofa: it is ACTIVELY moving, not still. Her side of the lump surges toward him; the whole shape leans and presses toward frame-LEFT as she pushes into him; his side tips back against the sofa's left arm and backrest; the fabric bunches and shifts; one of his socked feet lifts and flexes at the hem in surprise; a muffled "mmph!" from him, then a muffled giggle from her. It reads clearly as her playfully overpowering him with a lip kiss. Only the blanket, its movement and their socked feet are visible: no faces, no skin, nothing explicit, no clothes coming off. The table props stay unmoved.
22–30s — THE ENDING. Stay in the same frontal shot from OUTSIDE the blanket (never cut underneath): she pulls the blanket down off her own head, hair slightly messed, the coral-orange claw clip still at the back-right. She looks at him with a slow, sexy, victorious smirk and wipes her lower lip once with her RIGHT thumb. Then the blanket slides off Image2's head too, in the same frontal angle, and he is revealed with his hair tousled: he is STUNNED — a subtle, realistic freeze: completely still, lips slightly parted, eyes a little wider, a slow blink. No hand over the mouth, no gasp, no exaggerated jaw drop, no cartoon reaction. Hold that stillness for about 1.5s. Then the corner of his mouth slowly lifts into a confident, knowing smirk back at her, eyes holding hers. Cut to Image3: her smirk instantly collapses, she gets shy and flustered, cheeks flushing, lets out a tiny embarrassed giggle and covers her whole face with BOTH hands, fingers together. Hold on her hiding her face, him still smirking beside her — then cut to black. No lines in this beat.

NEGATIVE
No extra voices: no narrator, voiceover, neighbor, TV dialogue you can understand, or third person. No dialogue other than the two lines; each line is spoken by the character on screen and in focus at that moment; the young male voice and the young female voice never merge or swap. No music, no BGM, no score, no instruments, no vocals, no swell. No visible TV, no visible screen, no readable image or logo in frame. No second blanket, no color-changing blanket, no duvet swap. No seat swap, no standing, no walking, no leaving the sofa, no shoulder lean, no sitting on a lap. No extra people, no pets. No rings on either right hand, no silver rings, no extra rings, no stones or engraving; gold wedding bands stay on the LEFT ring fingers only. No explicit nudity, no clothes removal, no under-blanket nudity or explicit touching; the covered-lump shot shows only the blanket, its clear pushing movement and their socked feet. No daylight, no morning, no lighting change between shots; the warm-amber lamp and the cool-blue TV-glow stay in every shot. No morphing faces or outfits; the coral-orange claw clip stays on at the back-right; the sage hoodie stays open; both stay in socks. Nobody drinks or eats or moves the table props. No grey blanket or second throw anywhere on the sofa; no blanket duplicating, shrinking, growing or changing check scale. The wedding frame never moves or turns black and no TV-like dark rectangle ever appears on the wall. No camera under the blanket. No feminine or high-pitched voice for Image2. No laugh track, audience or background chatter. No cooler or bluer lighting in close-ups. No scrunchie or fuzzy hair tie; the coral claw clip stays the same. No ring on an index finger; no warped ring. No backwards or wrong-sided hands. No theatrical sneeze. No jump cuts that break posture continuity. No text, subtitles, watermark or UI. No fast cuts, shaky handheld or fast-forward look. No screaming, no cartoon faces beyond a live-action fake sneeze and a cute grumpy glare. NO single one-pull tug: the tug-of-war must visibly go back and forth at least three times with a taut blanket. NO motionless lump under the blanket: the covered shape must visibly push, lean and shift toward his side. NO overacted surprise at the end: he does not cover his mouth, gasp, drop his jaw or widen his eyes dramatically; he freezes subtly, then smirks. She covers her face with both hands at the very end.
```

</details>

THE DRAGON WARRIOR — GOLDEN HARBOR AMBUSH

ENGINE DIRECTIVE: Create an exactly 30-second, 16:9, photorealistic Hollywood martial-arts action sequence, targeting 60 FPS. Use @image1 as protagonist identity, @image2 as ninja antagonist and @image3 as harbor environment.

CHARACTER CONTINUITY:
@image1: Preserve exact face, beard, hairstyle, skin tone and body proportions. Outfit: deep-teal martial-arts tunic, black leather armor with embossed golden dragon emblem, black shoulder guards and bracers, teal trousers, black combat boots and traditional conical bamboo hat. Bare-handed fighter.

@image2: Eight masked black-clad ninja assassins. Their leader wears layered charcoal shinobi robes, black leather armor, reinforced guards, cross-body straps, black mask and conical hat. He carries a curved steel katana with black handle and scabbard. Maintain identical appearances.

@image3: Ancient wooden harbor surrounded by misty mountains, traditional sailing ships, beige sails, turquoise dock posts, cargo crates and wooden market stalls. Golden sunrise, atmospheric mist and reflective water. Maintain exact geography.

AUDIO: Original Asian cinematic action music with bamboo flute, strings and powerful drums. Add synchronized punches, kicks, sword swishes, footsteps, impacts, harbor ambience and ship creaks.

TIMELINE

00:00–00:03 | AMBUSH
Wide harbor reveal. Warrior walks along the narrow pier as masked ninjas emerge. Low-angle cinematic tracking.

00:03–00:06 | FIRST ATTACK
A ninja charges. Warrior blocks with his forearm and punches the attacker in the ribs, knocking him down.

00:06–00:09 | SIDE KICK
Second ninja attacks. Warrior delivers a powerful side kick, sending him backward.

00:09–00:12 | SURROUNDED
Three ninjas attack together. Warrior catches a leaping enemy's leg and throws him onto the wooden pier.

00:12–00:15 | SWORDS DRAWN
Camera pushes toward the wider cargo platform. Remaining ninjas draw curved swords. Leader reveals his katana.

00:15–00:18 | BLADE COMBAT
Leader swings at the warrior. He ducks, controls the weapon wrist and punches the attacker in the torso.

00:18–00:21 | COUNTERATTACK
Another swordsman attacks from behind. Warrior pivots, avoids the blade and throws him against a cargo crate.

00:21–00:24 | FINAL THROW
Leader charges again. Warrior redirects his sword arm and performs a powerful rotational hip throw. Katana slides across the deck.

00:24–00:27 | VICTORY
Wide shot of unconscious ninjas across the wooden platform. Warrior stands confidently against the golden sunrise.

00:27–00:30 | FINAL STANCE
Slow cinematic push-in. Warrior enters a low martial-arts stance, looks toward camera and says: "Watch out for this one." Dramatic drum impact, fade to black at exactly 30 seconds.

CONTINUITY: Preserve all reference identities, costumes, weapons and environment. Realistic martial-arts physics, natural movement, cinematic lighting and consistent geography. No morphing, teleportation, duplicated limbs, gore, cartoon visuals, subtitles or watermarks.
```

### 直升机追踪洛杉矶街头越野摩托特技骑手

> Perfect AI cinematography is overrated. Sometimes you need shaky tracking, missed framing, ugly zooms and compression artifacts to make something feel REAL. 👀 …

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/68b308e11fb0.jpg" width="600" alt="直升机追踪洛杉矶街头越野摩托特技骑手">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-perfect-ai-cinematography-is-overrated-26e640ef7c4f?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @CharaspowerAI | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/CharaspowerAI/status/2111517673276240251) | **发布:** 2026-10-17 | **热度:** 67

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（26 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Perfect AI cinematography is overrated.
Sometimes you need shaky tracking, missed framing, ugly zooms and compression artifacts to make something feel REAL. 👀

This Seedance 2.5 experiment proves it.
Full prompt below 👇
Ultra-realistic viral helicopter pursuit footage, raw local-news style, filmed entirely from one helicopter in bright Los Angeles daylight. Long-lens aerial camera, unstable vibration, abrupt zooms, compression artifacts, heat haze, autofocus breathing, imperfect framing, rotor thump, distant radio chatter, harsh California sun, no cinematic polish, believable amateur-news realism.

Main subject: one motocross rider on a dirt bike, full helmet and motocross gear, highly skilled, aggressive but controlled riding. Keep the same rider and same bike throughout.

Los Angeles daytime: palm-lined boulevard, parking lots, freeway ramps, concrete flood channel, industrial blocks, overpasses, service roads and distant skyline.

0-5s: Immediate viral hook The helicopter camera is already searching frantically when the rider suddenly blasts into frame between moving cars. Operator snaps into a shaky zoom. The rider jumps a curb, cuts across a parking lot and nearly disappears behind palm trees.

5-10s: Chaotic tracking He rejoins a boulevard, threads through traffic, then veers up a sloped driveway and launches over a low divider. Camera over-zooms, loses him for half a second, then catches him landing cleanly.

10-15s: Escalation The rider enters a concrete flood channel. Huge aerial reveal. He accelerates along the channel floor while the helicopter banks hard overhead. Dust trails behind the bike. Quick zoom on spinning rear wheel and suspension.

15-20s: Viral stunt beat He rides up the angled concrete wall, holds a dramatic high line for a moment, then drops back down smoothly. Camera shakes violently trying to follow. Rotor vibration and radio chatter intensify.

20-25s: Big surprise The rider exits through a service ramp, crosses beneath an overpass, then unexpectedly appears on a parallel industrial road. The operator pans late and barely recovers him. He hits a natural rise and gets long airtime in full daylight.

25-30s: Iconic ending He lands, accelerates toward another overpass and vanishes into shadow underneath. The helicopter overshoots, circles, zooms wildly across multiple roads, but he is gone. Reporter voice, stunned: “We just lost him.” Hold on shaky wide aerial of Los Angeles. Hard cut.

One continuous helicopter perspective, strong amateur-news authenticity, same rider throughout, realistic motocross physics, believable Los Angeles geography, harsh daylight, imperfect tracking, viral unpredictability, readable stunts, no crash, no gore, no readable text, no logos.

#DreaminaCPP  @dreamina_ai
```

</details>

22–26s

26–30s

```
TITLE: "What Falls from the Plum Tree?"
DURATION: 15 seconds | ASPECT RATIO: 16:9
GENRE: Romantic Japanese Fantasy Comedy
STYLE: Ultra-realistic premium Japanese drama, cinematic film quality

IDENTITY LOCK:
The woman @image1 must keep the exact same face, face shape, skin tone, hairstyle, height, body proportions, and wardrobe in every shot. The man @image2 must keep his exact face, hairstyle, and outfit as in the reference. No changes in any frame.

ENVIRONMENT LOCK:
An elegant Edo-era Japanese shrine courtyard with traditional wooden architecture, tiled rooftops, stone lanterns, and a raked gravel garden in the background. A giant ancient plum (ume) tree with dense white and pink blossoms. Its thick main branch extends horizontally above the woman's head. Soft spring daylight.

SHOT BREAKDOWN:

0.0–4.0s | Medium shot
The woman stands beneath the huge horizontal branch. Blossom petals drift in the breeze. She looks up curiously, then raises both hands and shakes the branch. The branch trembles and a shower of petals falls. A lacquered cosmetic box tumbles down through the blossoms and she catches it with both hands. Close-up of her face as she peeks inside. Flat, unimpressed expression. She tosses it casually out of frame.

4.0–7.5s | Medium shot
Her curiosity grows. She shakes the branch harder and petals pour down like rain. A scatter of gold koban coins and a few silk pouches fall. She catches one pouch, raises a single eyebrow, looks bored, and tosses it away.

7.5–10.5s | Slow motion
Determined now, she grips the branch with both hands and shakes with all her strength. The branch shudders, a heavy blossom storm rains down, and the man @image2 suddenly drops from the branch. She gasps, spreads her arms, and catches him just before he hits the ground. Petals swirl around them.

10.5–13.0s | Close-up, 50mm
She looks at his face. Her eyes sparkle and a wide, delighted smile appears. He gives a charming, slightly embarrassed smile and looks back at her.

13.0–15.0s | Medium-wide shot
They turn to the camera together and make small heart shapes with their hands, laughing happily. They turn to each other, laugh, and walk out of frame side by side while petals keep falling behind them. Fade out on the falling blossoms.

EMOTION ARC:
Curious → Bored → More curious → Surprised → Smitten → Happy.

SOUND:
Soft spring breeze, rustling blossoms, creaking wood, light thud of the box, jingle of coins, warm playful Japanese rom-com music (koto, light shamisen, soft piano), cheerful laughter, fading footsteps.

CAMERA & VISUALS:
ARRI Alexa 35 look, 35mm and 50mm cinematic lenses, smooth gimbal movement, slow-motion petal shower, natural spring lighting, realistic skin pores, physically accurate shadows, HDR, subtle film grain, soft pastel color grading.

NEGATIVE PROMPT:
cartoon, anime, CGI look, low quality, blurry face, extra fingers, warped hands, duplicate objects, duplicate people, distorted body, floating objects, unrealistic physics, unnatural falling motion, overexposed lighting, oversaturated colors, AI artifacts, camera shake, abrupt cuts, warped architecture, deformed eyes

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（21 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic early-2000s DV home video of a young Korean woman doing quiet Sunday chores around her home in an older Seoul neighborhood. Purely observational, no dialogue, no narrative arc  just rhythmic, textural everyday realism.
MAIN SUBJECT
Young Korean woman same as , early 20s, natural beauty, realistic skin texture, no makeup, black hair tied up messily in a claw clip, oversized faded T-shirt, rolled-up sweatpants, bare feet or house slippers. Keep her identity, outfit, hairstyle and proportions perfectly consistent throughout.
SETTING
The front step and small yard/alley outside an older Seoul home. Concrete step, a low gate, potted plants, a garden hose, a trash bin, scattered leaves. Ordinary, lived-in, no landmarks or brand names.
SEQUENCE (rhythmic, no dialogue)
She sweeps the front step with a short broom, dust and leaves scattering in the light.
She crouches down and pours a small amount of dry food into a bowl for a neighborhood cat waiting nearby; the cat eats while she watches briefly.
She stands and waters potted plants along the wall with a small watering can, water visibly darkening the soil.
She picks up a bagged trash bundle and carries it a few steps to place it by the curb/bin.
She wipes her hands on her shirt, looks around the now-tidy step and yard for a moment, then exhales/stretches.
She glances toward the camera briefly with a small, tired smile — no words — then turns and walks back inside.
Camera holds on the empty, swept step for a beat before cutting to black.
CAMERA
Raw handheld early-2000s DV camcorder, shaky footage, autofocus hunting, faded colors, exposure shifts, motion blur, soft digital detail, occasional accidental zooms. Observational, documentary-like framing — lingering shots, no cuts within actions. No polished cinematography.
AUDIO
Natural ambience only — broom bristles on concrete, water pouring, cat eating, distant birds, wind, a far-off scooter or dog bark, plastic bag rustling, faint footsteps. No dialogue, no music.
CONTINUITY
Broom, watering can and trash bag each remain consistent through their respective actions. Cat stays in frame while eating, doesn't disappear or duplicate. No teleporting or vanishing objects. Smooth, realistic, unhurried movement throughout.
NEGATIVE
CGI look, plastic skin, identity drift, outfit changes, distorted hands, extra fingers, subtitles, watermark.
```

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（24 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second photorealistic cinematic laundry commercial featuring a young South Asian woman in a bright, modern home. Include a natural female voiceover throughout the video, synchronized with the actions. The voice should sound warm, friendly, clear, and conversational, with soft realistic background laundry sounds and no music overpowering the voice.

0–5 seconds: The woman walks into the laundry area carrying a basket full of dirty clothes and places it beside a modern front-loading washing machine.
Voiceover: “Laundry day starts with one simple step.”

5–10 seconds: She opens the washing machine and carefully puts the clothes inside one by one. Show realistic fabric movement and natural hand movements.
Voiceover: “I just load all my clothes into the washing machine…”

10–13 seconds: She adds liquid detergent into the detergent compartment, closes it, and shuts the washing machine door.
Voiceover: “…add my detergent…”

13–17 seconds: She presses the start button. Close-up of the washing machine starting, with the clothes visibly spinning behind the glass door.
Voiceover: “…and let the machine do the work.”

17–22 seconds: After the wash, she opens the machine and takes out the fresh, clean clothes. She gently shakes and unfolds the garments.
Voiceover: “Once they’re clean, they come out fresh and ready to dry.”

22–28 seconds: She carries the clean clothes outside to a clothesline and hangs each garment carefully using clothespins. A gentle breeze makes the clothes move naturally.
Voiceover: “Then I simply hang them outside and let the fresh air do the rest.”

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（71 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
CHARACTER SHEETS — FICTIONAL, TEXT-ONLY (NO PHOTO REFERENCE):

MAYA: Spanish, mid-20s, around 5'6", slim build. Black hair, straight and glossy, worn in a slightly messy high ponytail with a few loose strands framing her face. Green eyes, straight dark eyebrows. Round glasses with a thin silver metal frame, always on. Small silver hoop earrings. Fair skin with natural texture and visible pores, a few faint freckles across the nose bridge only (much lighter than Sofia's). No tattoos, no visible marks or piercings beyond ears. Calm, dry facial expressions — she rarely smiles broadly, mostly small smirks.

NADIA: mid-20s, around 5'7", athletic build. Long, straight, electric-blue hair with visible dark roots, usually worn down and slightly tousled. Blue-grey eyes, strong dark eyebrows, full lips. Fair-to-olive skin, smooth, no visible tattoos. Minimal jewelry — small black stud earrings. Intense, guarded expression by default; rarely smiles, and when she does it's brief and subtle.

SOFIA: mid-20s, around 5'5", curvier build. Wavy, golden-blonde hair falling just past the shoulders, worn loose and natural. Blue-grey eyes, warm expressive eyebrows. Noticeable freckles scattered across her nose and both cheeks. Wide, easy, genuine smile — she's the most expressive and animated of the three. Gold small hoop earrings. Fair skin with a light golden undertone from sun exposure.

EXCLUSIVE TRAITS — LOCKED, NEVER TRANSFER:

Only Maya wears glasses and a high ponytail.
Only Nadia has blue hair.
Only Sofia has visible freckles and blonde hair.

THE FESTIVAL: Ibiza open-air electronic festival, white architecture — curved white sculptural stage shell, white canopies and candles, real palm trees, polished concrete, warm white-and-gold ambient light, white railings, no readable branding.

CAST RULE — FIXED: exactly these three women appear with visible faces throughout. No fourth character. No men. Background crowd and staff are always blurred or silhouetted, never in sharp focus.

WARDROBE:
Shots 1–4 (street, planning): casual streetwear.

Maya: oversized denim jacket over a plain white t-shirt, black jeans, white sneakers.
Nadia: black hoodie, black cargo pants, black combat boots.
Sofia: cropped cardigan over a tank top, denim shorts, white sneakers.

Shots 5–8 (backstage/onstage): festival-appropriate, consistent per character, colors never swapped.

Maya: silver sequin top, silver metallic accents, glasses still on.
Nadia: black mesh/metallic detail top, dark tones throughout.
Sofia: gold sequin top, warm metallic tones.

FORMAT: 30 seconds / 8 shots / horizontal 16:9. Cinematic realism, tight composed framing, zero wide or establishing shots. 85mm-equivalent lens, shallow depth of field, clean bokeh. Each shot one continuous take, no internal cuts, no zoom. Real-time speed, no slow motion. Natural spoken English, dialogue starts immediately in Shot 1.

STYLE: A24-style cinematic realism, golden hour for Part 1 transitioning to festival stage lighting for Part 2 — warm side light, long shadows, lens flares, high contrast, warm natural color grade, light 35mm film grain. Realistic skin texture throughout, no smoothing filter, no CGI shine.

MUSIC: low-key instrumental house track, distant and muted in Part 1 (as if bleeding from the festival), swelling in volume and presence through Part 2, no lyrics, never overpowering dialogue.

PART 1 — THE PLAN (0:00–0:15)

SHOT 1 (0:00–0:04): Medium shot, eye-level. Three on a concrete bench outside the sealed white perimeter fence, slouched, bored. Nadia left, Maya center, Sofia right. Static take.
Nadia, flat, bored: "What if we sneak into the festival?"
SFX: distant bass, crowd murmur, a car passing.

SHOT 2 (0:04–0:07.5): Tighter medium on Sofia, others soft-blurred at edges. Slight push-in as her expression brightens.
Sofia: "Can we pretend we're DJs?"
SFX: bass continues, light wind.

SHOT 3 (0:07.5–0:11): Medium shot on Maya, static, sideways deadpan glance.
Maya, dry: "With what equipment, genius?"
SFX: bass, a stifled laugh off-frame.

SHOT 4 (0:11–0:15): All three lean in, energy shifting. Maya stands.
Maya, calm: "Okay. Let's go."
SFX: fabric rustle, footsteps starting, bass rising.

PART 2 — THE RUSH (0:15–0:30)

SHOT 5 (0:15–0:18): Shoulder-height handheld, behind the three as they run through a narrow backstage corridor between equipment cases. No dialogue.
SFX: footsteps on rubber matting, breathless breathing, rising crowd roar.

SHOT 6 (0:18–0:21): Low-angle handheld on a metal staircase, the three climbing toward stage light, silhouetted from above.
Nadia, breathless: "What now?!"
SFX: metal footsteps, crowd growing louder.

SHOT 7 (0:21–0:26): Medium shot at the DJ booth, hands landing on the mixing table, light flaring up from below. Sofia grabs the mic.
Sofia, shouting with unearned confidence: "WE MADE IT! MAKE SOME NOISE!"
SFX: her voice echoing through the sound system, massive crowd roar.

SHOT 8 (0:26–0:30): All three in the booth, arms raised, laughing, genuinely ecstatic, blurred lights and haze behind them. No dialogue. Hard cut to black.

NEGATIVE PROMPT: any man in frame with a visible face, a fourth character, sharply focused crowd faces, glasses on anyone but Maya, blue hair on Maya, freckles on Nadia, wardrobe color swaps between characters, slow motion, wide/establishing/aerial shots, flat/studio lighting, characters looking at camera, zooming, mid-shot cuts, smoothing filters, distorted hands/faces, extra fingers, subtitles, on-screen text, watermarks, overpowering music, lyrics in the track.
```

**作者:** @AIwithkhan | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/AIwithkhan/status/2123306748694114466) | **发布:** 2026-11-19 | **热度:** 65

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（13 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Create an ultra-realistic 30-second live-action revenge-comedy scene inside a luxurious shopping mall food court in Seoul, South Korea. Natural handheld camera movement, bright indoor lighting, realistic crowd reactions, expressive facial performances, and fast-paced Korean action-comedy cinematography. The footage must look like a real movie, not animation or a video game.
CHARACTERS:
Woman: A stylish Korean woman in her 20s with a short black bob haircut, wearing a fitted pink crop top, high-waisted light-blue jeans, fashionable black boots, and a small black shoulder bag. She carries a large red-and-white popcorn bucket and a smartphone.
Man: A Korean man in his 20s with messy dark hair featuring blonde highlights, wearing a black leather jacket, white T-shirt, and dark trousers.
ACTION SEQUENCE — EXACTLY 30 SECONDS:
0–5 seconds: Establish the busy shopping mall food court. The woman casually walks past restaurants, eating popcorn and checking her phone. People are chatting in the background. The camera follows her naturally.
5–9 seconds: A man suddenly approaches and aggressively slaps her across the face as he passes. He turns around laughing mockingly. Her popcorn bucket flies out of her hands, scattering popcorn across the floor. Her smartphone drops and slides underneath a nearby table. She stumbles backward, holding her cheek, looking at him in disbelief.
9–13 seconds: She slowly turns toward him. Her facial expression changes from shock to cold anger. She picks up the fallen popcorn bucket, grabs a handful of popcorn, and suddenly throws it directly into his face. Popcorn explodes across his hair, eyes, and leather jacket. His laughter immediately stops.
13–19 seconds: The embarrassed man angrily charges toward her. She quickly dodges, grabs the front of his leather jacket, pivots, and performs a dramatic but physically convincing action-comedy throw. He tumbles backward into a large decorative laundry collection bin beside a pillar. The bin rocks violently. Nearby shoppers gasp in surprise.
19–24 seconds: She approaches the bin calmly, looks down at him, and says in a firm, icy voice: "Next time, watch where you're going." She grabs the metal lid and slams it shut. A loud metallic CLANG echoes through the food court.
24–30 seconds: She walks back, retrieves her smartphone from underneath the table, checks the screen, brushes popcorn off her pink crop top, adjusts her shoulder bag, and confidently walks away. In the background, the bin lid slowly opens, revealing the man's shocked face covered in popcorn. He tries to escape, but the lid falls shut again with a final metallic CLANG. End with a cinematic shot of the woman walking away without looking back.
SOUND DESIGN: Authentic shopping mall ambience, distant conversations, footsteps, sudden slap impact, phone sliding across the floor, mocking laughter, popcorn scattering, wet and crunchy popcorn impact, shoes scraping, crowd gasps, heavy bin impact, synchronized dialogue, and metallic lid slams. No overpowering background music.
VISUAL REQUIREMENTS: Total duration must be exactly 30 seconds. Preserve consistent faces, hairstyles, clothing, and location throughout all scenes. Every action must happen in chronological order. Clearly show the slap, falling phone, scattered popcorn, popcorn hitting his face, complete throwing action, and bin lid closing. Use readable continuous action shots, realistic body physics, natural facial expressions, detailed skin textures, cinematic lighting, and authentic live-action footage. No gore, no supernatural powers, no cartoon physics, no animation, and no skipped actions.
```

</details>

**作者:** @Xaroon_x | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/Xaroon_x/status/2123291707462807715) | **发布:** 2026-11-19 | **热度:** 65

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（63 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
THE FOREST GUARDIAN — CapCut SEEDANCE 2.5 4K. 

Create an exactly 30-second, photorealistic Hollywood action sequence, 16:9 widescreen, 60fps target, with cinematic camera movements, realistic stunts, extreme macro shots and dramatic slow motion.

VISUAL REFERENCES

@image1: Preserve protagonist's exact face, hairstyle, skin tone, facial features and body proportions. No identity changes.

@image2: Four consistent male forest bandits with distinct faces and outfits:
Bandit 1: Muscular, brown leather vest, black shirt, messy hair.
Bandit 2: Heavy muscular build, beard, sleeveless charcoal leather vest.
Bandit 3: Lean build, grey shirt, brown waistcoat.
Bandit 4: Stocky build, olive-brown hooded jacket.

@image3: Dense beautiful green forest, natural woodland clearing, giant oak tree on the right, golden afternoon sunlight, moss, dry leaves and realistic forest atmosphere.

PROTAGONIST OUTFIT

Dark forest-green leather ranger jacket, charcoal shirt, brown leather archery gloves, dark rugged trousers, brown wilderness boots, leather utility belt and back-mounted quiver. Traditional dark walnut wooden recurve bow with black-feathered arrows.

WOMAN

Beautiful adult woman wearing a modest cream-beige outfit and brown scarf, carrying a tan leather shoulder bag.

TIMELINE

00:00–00:03 | ROBBERY
Wide cinematic forest reveal. Four bandits surround the frightened woman and aggressively pull her shoulder bag. Close-up of her frightened face.

00:03–00:06 | HERO REVEAL
Jump cut to protagonist's extreme facial close-up using @image1. He hides behind a tree, retrieves an arrow and draws his wooden bow.

00:06–00:09 | ARROW SLOW MOTION
Extreme macro of fingers releasing the bowstring. Show vibrating strings, flexing bow limbs, spinning arrow, feather details and metallic arrowhead. Cinematic tracking follows its flight through sunlight. Arrow strikes Bandit 1's upper shoulder. He falls.

00:09–00:12 | HEROIC JUMP
Remaining bandits turn. Protagonist sprints toward them. Low-angle tracking shot transitions into slow motion as he jumps and extends his leg for an airborne side kick.

00:12–00:15 | IMPACT
Extreme slow-motion close-up of boot contacting Bandit 2. Wide side shot shows him crashing against the large oak tree and falling.

00:15–00:18 | FIGHT BEGINS
Bandit 3 attacks. Hero blocks his punch, catches his arm and counterattacks.

00:18–00:21 | FINAL FIGHT
Hero dodges, pivots and performs realistic martial arts takedowns against Bandits 3 and 4. Dynamic tracking, close-up impacts and scattering leaves.

00:21–00:24 | VICTORY
Low-angle hero shot. All four bandits defeated in their established positions. Close-up of protagonist's unchanged face.

00:24–00:27 | RESCUE
Hero picks up the original tan leather bag and respectfully returns it to the woman.

00:27–00:30 | FINAL SHOT
Woman receives her bag with relief. Hero gives a reassuring nod. Camera slowly pulls back, revealing the beautiful golden forest and both characters.

AUDIO

Original Hollywood orchestral action music, tense strings, powerful cinematic drums, realistic bowstring snap, arrow whistle, footsteps, clothing movement, martial arts impacts, tree collision, leaves and natural forest ambience. Finish with emotional piano and orchestral strings.

CONTINUITY AND NEGATIVE PROMPT

Maintain exact character identities, costumes, weapons, bag and forest geography. Realistic anatomy, weight, momentum and stunt physics. Use slow motion selectively for arrow release, arrow flight, impact, jump and kick. No face changes, duplicated characters, distorted limbs, teleportation, floating bodies, cartoon effects, artificial CGI appearance, random costume changes, excessive motion blur, text, logos or watermarks.
```

</details>

```
ENGINE DIRECTIVE

Genre: 1930s British gangster noir, cinematic crime-action thriller.

Duration: EXACTLY 30 seconds, 16:9 widescreen, 60fps target, one continuous generation.

Style: Ultra-realistic Hollywood cinematography, vintage London atmosphere, dramatic slow motion, practical VFX.

REFERENCE LOCK

- @image1 = Protagonist identity.
- @image2 = Three antagonists.
- @image3 = 1930s London pub and street.

VISUAL ASSETS

PROTAGONIST — @image1: Preserve exact face, curly hairstyle, beard, skin tone and body structure. Outfit: charcoal-black pinstripe three-piece suit, white shirt, burgundy tie, long dark wool overcoat, black leather Oxford shoes and charcoal 1930s newsboy cap. Vintage silver watch. Cigarette and compact 1930s blued-steel pistol.

ANTAGONISTS — @image2: Three distinct gangsters: (1) Stocky scarred enforcer in brown overcoat, (2) tall moustached gangster in navy suit and black coat, (3) athletic young gunman in olive-green coat. Each carries a vintage pistol. Preserve their individual appearances.

ENVIRONMENT — @image3: Authentic 1930s Soho pub with mahogany bar, burgundy booths, amber lamps and smoky interior. Outside: narrow Victorian red-brick street, wet cobblestones, black vintage automobile, iron railings, warm streetlights and drifting London fog.

AUDIO: Original dark vintage jazz with muted trumpet, piano and bass. Realistic footsteps, cigarette sounds, metallic weapon handling, synchronized gunshots, shell casings, brick impacts and cinematic echoes.

TIMELINE

00:00–00:03 | THE STRANGER: Wide shot of protagonist sitting alone in a smoky pub booth, casually smoking. Warm amber lighting. Slow cinematic push-in.

00:03–00:06 | THE THREE HUNTERS: Heavy door opens. Three rival gangsters enter, scanning the pub for him. Low-angle tracking shot.

00:06–00:09 | HE SEES THEM: Extreme close-up of protagonist's eyes. He notices them in the mirror, finishes his cigarette, adjusts his tie and calmly walks outside.

00:09–00:12 | THE PURSUIT: Protagonist exits into the foggy street. All three gangsters recognize him and follow.

00:12–00:15 | READY FOR WAR: Beside a brick wall, protagonist retrieves his pistol. All three enemies draw their weapons. Dramatic close-ups.

00:15–00:18 | FIRST EXPLOSION: Sudden gunfire. Protagonist fires first and takes cover. 120fps-style slow motion, orange muzzle flashes, shell casings, brick debris and smoke.

00:18–00:21 | STREET WAR: Intense crossfire from different camera angles. The scarred enforcer is struck and collapses beside the automobile.

00:21–00:24 | TWO MORE: Dynamic profile tracking. The protagonist defeats the tall moustached gangster. The young gunman attempts to reposition near the car.

00:24–00:27 | THE FINAL SHOT: Extreme close-up, dramatic slow motion and one final gunshot. The last gangster falls. All three enemies are defeated. Restrained action-movie impacts, no excessive gore.

00:27–00:30 | THE COLD EXIT: Protagonist lowers his pistol, puts it beneath his coat, takes out another cigarette and lights it with a silver lighter. He exhales smoke and walks away into the London fog. Rear tracking shot.

FINAL DIRECTIVE: Maintain exact character identities, outfits, weapons and location continuity. Realistic choreography, cinematic gunfire VFX, slow motion, volumetric fog, dramatic lighting, film grain and premium Hollywood color grading. No character duplication, teleportation or excessive gore.

FINAL MOOD: One gangster. Three enemies. No fear. Just another cigarette break in Old London.
```

</details>

```
Create a ultra-realistic early-2000s consumer DV camcorder home video of a young Korean woman enjoying a simple day in an older Seoul residential neighborhood. Use the attached image as the character reference and keep her face and appearance consistent. She wears a pink fitted crop top, white casual shorts, simple slippers, and a small bracelet, carrying a clear water bottle.

She steps out of her home, closes the door and walks through the quiet neighborhood with the bottle in hand. Along the way, she greets a few friendly neighbors with natural smiles and small waves.

She reaches a neighborhood park and sits on a bench, casually watching children playing football. Suddenly, the football rolls toward her. She picks it up, smiles, kicks it gently back to the children and says, “Enjoy!” The children laugh and continue playing.

She leaves the park and walks down the street, still carrying her water bottle. She stops at a small street vendor selling sweet corn, buys one and happily takes her first bite while walking.

While eating, her water bottle accidentally slips from her hand and falls onto the sidewalk. She pauses, looks down, laughs softly, picks it up, takes another bite of the sweet corn and continues walking home.

Near the end, she walks slowly down the residential lane with the water bottle in one hand and sweet corn in the other, enjoying her snack. She looks toward the camera, smiles naturally and says “Bye bye!”, then walks away.

Use raw early-2000s consumer DV-camera footage: handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, slight exposure shifts, soft digital detail, mild digital noise, natural motion blur and occasional awkward zooms. Authentic home-video feeling, realistic skin, natural expressions and believable movement.

Natural neighborhood audio only: footsteps, birds, distant traffic, children playing football, neighbors talking, bicycle sounds, vendor ambience, water bottle movement and light wind. No music, narration, subtitles, beauty filters, CGI, cinematic commercial look or polished camera movement.
```

</details>
