**作者:** @ElsaSofia__AI | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/ElsaSofia__AI/status/2100179633266418072) | **发布:** 2026-09-16 | **热度:** 65

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（77 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
⤵️ 
SCENE 1 (0:00–0:30) — MONOCHROME STUDIO → FULL RITUAL → EMERALD GOWN → LAST DETAIL
SHOT STRUCTURE: 15 shots, 30 seconds, 16:9
@Image1 is the Aiko character reference — keep her face, fair skin, sleek black bob haircut and dark-brown eyes identical in every shot.
GLOBAL STYLE NOTES:
lighting_philosophy: crisp white beauty-dish key on her face, cool silver glow from square-framed vanity mirrors behind, soft grey practicals on walls; glossy speculars on marble, chrome and glass.
color_grade: high-gloss monochrome beauty commercial — charcoal-grey walls, ivory marble, fair skin held clean and cool; the emerald gown is the only deep saturated green in the film.
setting: sleek minimalist glam studio — charcoal-grey walls, polished ivory marble floor, low velvet stools in ash-grey, white sheepskin rugs, three large square chrome-framed vanity mirrors, a thin white LED strip along the ceiling, beauty-dish lights on stands, a disco ball throwing slow silver dots across the marble, open shelves of matte black labelled bottles and jars, a single orchid in a glass vase, a chrome drinks cart.
staff: three women in charcoal uniforms — an esthetician, a make-up artist, a hair stylist. Shown mainly as hands, forearms, backs and soft-focus bodies. No close-ups of their faces except the final reaction in medium shot. They move fast and react openly.
mirror_rule: mirrors stay BEHIND or BESIDE her, soft-focus, as light and texture only. Never a sharp reflection of her face shown next to her actual face in the same frame.
characters:
Aiko: visual_anchors: petite elegant build, sleek jet-black bob haircut with blunt bangs, dark-brown eyes, fair even skin, a small mole near one eye. Opens in a plain white ribbed tank top. Performance: composed, quietly amused — one mouth corner lifts, never a wide expression. She is the only still person in a room full of motion.
ambience: glossy pop beat locked to every cut, studio chatter under it, hairdryer hum. Beat drops at shot 12, cuts to silence at shot 15.
SEQUENCE LIST:
SHOT 1 (0–2s) HOOK — WS, glass door
camera_motion: static, then fast push-in
action_visual: Aiko (per @Image1) pushes the glass door open in a plain white tank top — cool silver light floods the frame. Door chime, beat starts on her step
exit: she walks past camera into the studio
SHOT 2 (2–4s) SETUP — MS tracking
camera_motion: handheld tracking behind her through the studio, chrome mirrors and shelves streaking past
action_visual: she drops into an ash-grey velvet stool. The esthetician snaps a white cape open and clicks it shut at her neck. A long emerald evening gown hangs soft-focus behind her; black leather ankle boots sit under the stool
exit: hands enter frame toward her face
SHOT 3 (4–6s) — insert macro on hands
camera_motion: static macro, 85mm
action_visual: a pump bottle pushes white cleansing foam into a gloved palm; gloved hands work the foam up her cheeks in one upward sweep. Wet foam squelch, pump hiss
exit: water enters frame
SHOT 4 (6–8s) — MCU
camera_motion: static, slight handheld float
action_visual: water rinses the foam, she lifts her wet face and droplets fly sideways catching the beauty-dish light
exit: a brush enters frame from the left
SHOT 5 (8–10s) SIGNATURE — MCU, frontal
vfx: none. Speed 100%
camera_motion: locked frontal, composition does not shift
action_visual: a brush lays charcoal clay mask in three strokes → jump to the mask already dry. She raises ONE eyebrow, the crust cracks across the bridge of her nose. The esthetician freezes mid-air with the brush. Aiko lowers the brow back
exit: two gel patches click under her eyes
SHOT 6 (10–12s) — MCU, frontal, same framing
camera_motion: locked, no movement
action_visual: the make-up artist slams a large powder puff straight into the lens, frame goes soft white → clears on glowing porcelain make-up and satin lips
exit: a brush sweeps across frame
SHOT 7 (12–14s) — MCU, same framing
camera_motion: locked
action_visual: the brush clears to reveal a sharp graphic black cat-eye liner and pale lips → a palm sweeps down the frame → clears on deep bronze eyelids
exit: the powder puff slams into the lens again
SHOT 8 (14–16s) — MCU, same framing
camera_motion: locked, tiny push-in
action_visual: frame clears on full smoky bronze eye, subtle highlighter on the cheekbones, deep berry-red lipstick. The make-up artist steps back and raises both hands. Aiko holds still half a second. Lipstick cap clicks
exit: a towel is pulled off her head
SHOT 9 (16–18s) — MS
camera_motion: static, then whip pan out and back
action_visual: the stylist rips the towel away, wet hair drops in one sheet → whip pan returns on a sleek glossy black bob shining under the beauty-dish light
exit: a flat iron enters frame
SHOT 10 (18–19s) — insert macro
vfx: speed ramp 50%→100%
camera_motion: static macro, 50mm
action_visual: the flat iron clicks open and releases a glossy sheet of hair, steam rises into the light beam
exit: steam crosses the lens
SHOT 11 (19–21s) — MS
camera_motion: two fast whip pans, out and back each time
action_visual: hair pushed back loose, the stylist tucks it behind her ear → whip pan → sleek blunt-bangs bob with a sharp edge. Aiko tips her head, hair swings across frame
exit: hair fills frame
SHOT 12 (21–24s) CLIMAX — MS to WS, studio floor
camera_motion: static, then slow pull-back
action_visual: the stylist lifts the emerald gown off a chrome hanger, the fabric flies across the whole frame — deep emerald floods the screen — then falls away: Aiko stands in the middle of the studio in a long emerald silk evening gown, bob restored, silk running to the floor. Silk whoosh, heavy fabric fall
exit: fingers rise to her ear
SHOT 13 (24–26s) — insert macro on ear
camera_motion: static macro, 85mm
action_visual: the make-up artist's fingers fasten a small emerald drop earring at her ear. The clasp click lands as the beat drop
exit: camera pulls wide
SHOT 14 (26–28s) — WS full length
camera_motion: static wide, three staff visible either side of her
action_visual: Aiko glances aside, takes two steps to the stool and bends down. Low insert: the emerald silk hem lifts and black leather ankle boots enter frame — one foot, then the other
exit: she straightens up
SHOT 15 (28–30s) PUNCHLINE — WS full length
camera_motion: locked off, no movement
action_visual: she straightens, adjusts the hem so the ankle boots stay visible, and looks straight into the lens. All three staff freeze. The stylist drops a brush — it clatters on the marble. One mouth corner lifts. She walks out of frame. Beat cuts to silence on the clatter
exit: empty studio, three women looking after her, brush on the floor. Hold one second, end
GENERATE FIRST: Shot 5 (eyebrow cracks the mask — the tone-setter), Shot 12 (emerald gown fabric wipe), Shot 15 (ankle boots punchline).
```

### 小公寓里的失控舞蹈练习

> Made with seedance 2.5 Prompt: Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic personal home-video of a young Korean woman con

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/media/poster/seedance-create-a-30-second-1080p-ultra-realistic-personal-home-video-of-a-young-korean-906969cda347.jpg" width="600" alt="小公寓里的失控舞蹈练习">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-create-a-30-second-1080p-ultra-realistic-personal-home-video-of-a-young-korean-906969cda347?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Ciri_ai | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/Ciri_ai/status/2095026480552271884) | **发布:** 2026-09-02 | **热度:** 65

**稳定度：** 76/100

**复测：** MiniMax H3 Max 768p · 2026-09-07 · ✅ 复现 (76 分) · [产物](https://media.goodcase.ai/retests/seedance-create-a-30-second-1080p-ultra-realistic-personal-home-video-of-a-young-korean-906969cda347/video-minimax-h3-768p-20260907-phase1/generated.mp4)

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（95 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic personal home-video of a young Korean woman confidently attempting to learn a viral TikTok dance trend in her tiny apartment, with her furniture and coordination working against her the entire time. No reference image.

**MAIN SUBJECT**

Young Korean woman in her early 20s, casual and determined energy, natural makeup, expression that shifts from focused confidence to flustered embarrassment.

Hair in a messy half-up bun, a few strands sticking to her face as she gets more flustered, small stud earrings.

Wearing an oversized band t-shirt tucked loosely into biker shorts, white ankle socks, no shoes, phone propped up against a stack of books on the floor to "record for practice."

Maintain the same face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and appearance throughout the entire video.

**SETTING**

A small, cluttered studio apartment living room, evening, warm lamp light mixed with the cool glow of a laptop screen playing the dance tutorial video.

A coffee table pushed slightly out of the way but not far enough, a floor lamp standing a little too close to her "stage area," a pile of laundry on the couch, a half-full water glass on the side table, string lights on the wall, and a roommate's bike leaning against the wall in the background. Cramped, real, an apartment clearly not built for dancing.

**CAMERA / VISUAL AESTHETIC**

Raw personal footage, filmed by her roommate on a phone propped up at first, then grabbed handheld once things go wrong — strong handheld vlog shake for the second half.

Static, slightly low-angle framing for the "practice" shots, mimicking a phone-on-a-stack-of-books setup. Frame is imperfectly centered, cutting off the top of her head slightly. Once the roommate grabs the phone, autofocus hunts, motion blur kicks in during the collision moment, exposure shifts as she moves between lamp light and shadow, visible digital noise in the dim room.

No stabilization, no gimbal smoothness, no cinematic camera moves — it should feel like a real phone recording that was never meant to be watched by anyone but her.

---

**00:00–00:05 — THE CONFIDENT SETUP**

She props her phone against a stack of books, steps back into frame, and shakes out her arms like an athlete warming up.

She points at the laptop screen playing the dance tutorial, then back at herself, deadly serious.

*"Okay. I've watched this like twenty times. I got this."*

**00:05–00:10 — FIRST EIGHT-COUNT**

The music starts. She nails the first few moves surprisingly well, sharp and confident, clearly proud of herself mid-move.

Roommate's voice from off-screen, genuinely impressed: *"Wait, okay—?!"*

The camera stays static, catching her in full frame, a rare clean moment.

**00:10–00:15 — THE FIRST WOBBLE**

A quick turn in the choreography catches her off guard — she spins a half-second late, arms flailing to catch up, and clips the edge of the coffee table with her shin.

She yelps, hopping on one foot, still trying to keep the rhythm going through gritted teeth.

*"I'm fine — I'm fine, keep going—"*

**00:15–00:20 — LAMP INCIDENT**

Recovering, she throws herself into the next big arm movement — her hand clips the floor lamp, sending it tilting dangerously.

Her roommate lunges into frame to catch it, grabbing the phone off the books in the process, camera suddenly handheld and shaking.

*"THE LAMP—"*

**00:20–00:25 — FULL CHAOS**

With the lamp saved but the camera now shaky and handheld, she tries to pick the choreography back up from memory, increasingly out of sync with the music, arms going in directions the dance never intended.

She knocks the water glass off the side table with her elbow, doesn't even stop, just grimaces and keeps dancing through the spill.

Roommate, laughing hysterically behind the now-unsteady camera: *"You're literally destroying the apartment—"*

**00:25–00:30 — FINAL MOMENT**

The song ends. She strikes a triumphant final pose — chest heaving, one hand on her hip, completely out of breath, water pooling near her sock.

She looks into the camera, dead serious despite the disaster around her.

*"...Post it. That was clean."*

Her roommate, still laughing, pans the camera slowly down to the spilled water and tipped lamp before swinging back to her unbothered face — and the footage cuts abruptly to black.

---

**AUDIO**

Natural location sound only:

The tutorial/dance track playing tinny from the laptop speakers, her footsteps and fabric movement, the thud of her shin hitting the table, the lamp wobbling and roommate's scramble to catch it, glass tipping and water spilling, her roommate's genuine off-camera laughter throughout, and handheld camera noise once it's picked up.

No added music beyond the in-scene laptop track. No narration. No artificial sound effects.

Spoken lines: *"Okay. I've watched this like twenty times. I got this."* / *"Wait, okay—?!"* (roommate) / *"I'm fine — I'm fine, keep going—"* / *"THE LAMP—"* (roommate) / *"You're literally destroying the apartment—"* (roommate) / *"...Post it. That was clean."*

**FINAL FEEL**

Chaotic, cramped-apartment energy — the universal experience of confidently trying a viral dance and immediately losing a fight with your own furniture. Funny, physical, unscripted-feeling, with a deadpan ending that lands harder because she genuinely doesn't acknowledge the destruction around her.

No exaggerated slapstick beyond real physical missteps. No professional dance skill required — the comedy comes from real momentum, real near-misses, and her total refusal to admit defeat.
```

</details>

```
30 seconds | 16:9 | Ultra-photorealistic live-action

Create a natural Korean summer day-in-the-life video featuring one young Korean woman spending a quiet afternoon preparing a simple chilled fruit snack at home.

Use the reference image only for her exact facial identity, hairstyle, skin tone, and body proportions. Keep her appearance consistent throughout the entire video.

OUTFIT: Lightweight pale-yellow short-sleeve blouse, loose white cotton pants, simple canvas shoulder bag, white low-profile sneakers, minimal silver jewelry. Outfit remains unchanged from beginning to end.

TIME & LIGHTING: Warm late-afternoon summer sunlight. Consistent weather, shadows, color temperature, and time of day across every scene.

00–05s — Neighborhood walk

She leaves a small Korean apartment building carrying her canvas bag and walks down a quiet residential street.

Trees move gently in the summer breeze. Parked scooters, apartment entrances, utility poles, bicycles, and distant pedestrians create an authentic neighborhood atmosphere.

She briefly looks at the camera and says:
“I need something refreshing today.”

05–10s — Local fruit stand

She arrives at a small neighborhood fruit shop with crates of peaches, grapes, melons and Korean pears.

Instead of immediately picking something, she walks around comparing the fruit.

She notices a basket of peaches, picks one up carefully, examines it, smells it, and smiles.

The shop owner says something casually off-camera.

She responds:
“This one looks perfect.”

She places several peaches into her canvas bag.

10–15s — Walking home

She walks back through the neighborhood carrying the slightly heavier bag.

The camera follows from behind and then moves beside her.

She adjusts the bag against her shoulder as the fruit shifts naturally inside.

A bicycle passes in the background. Leaves move gently in the breeze. Her hair moves subtly with the air.

15–21s — Apartment kitchen

She enters her apartment kitchen and places the peaches on the counter.

She turns on the faucet and rinses them under cool running water.

Water droplets collect on the fruit and fall naturally into the sink.

She dries one peach with a clean kitchen towel and places it onto a small ceramic plate.

21–26s — Preparing the snack

She sits at the kitchen counter.

Using a small kitchen knife, she carefully slices the peach in half.

The knife meets realistic resistance before passing through the soft fruit.

She removes the pit, cuts several thin slices, and arranges them casually on the plate.

Close-up of realistic juice and peach texture.

26–30s — First bite

She picks up one slice and takes her first bite.

She pauses for a moment, smiles naturally, and quietly says:

“So good.”

She looks toward the window as warm sunlight falls across the kitchen.

The camera slowly drifts backward, capturing the cozy kitchen, open window, gentle curtain movement, and late-summer atmosphere.

Cut naturally at the end.

CAMERA STYLE: Documentary lifestyle footage with smooth handheld movement. Natural tracking shots, occasional subtle camera sway, realistic focus transitions, restrained depth of field, natural lens behavior, and authentic perspective. Avoid overly cinematic shots or commercial advertising aesthetics.

AUDIO: Real environmental audio only — distant neighborhood sounds, footsteps, bicycle passing, fruit shop conversations, plastic bags, fruit being handled, apartment door, running water, knife against cutting board, refrigerator hum, subtle curtain movement, breathing, and natural eating sounds. No background music.

PHYSICAL REALISM: The shopping bag reacts naturally to the weight of the fruit. Peach movement follows gravity and momentum. Hands maintain believable grip and pressure. Water flows naturally. Knife movement shows realistic cutting resistance. All body movements, walking, reaching, lifting, sitting, washing, cutting, and eating should feel spontaneous and physically accurate.

CONTINUITY: Same woman, facial identity, hairstyle, body proportions, outfit, bag, fruit, apartment, kitchen, weather, lighting, and time of day throughout. No unexplained object changes.

NEGATIVE PROMPT: No CGI look, synthetic skin, beauty filter, excessive sharpening, HDR, artificial blur, unrealistic depth of field, robotic movement, weightless objects, floating fruit, impossible hand positions, distorted anatomy, extra fingers, malformed hands, face drift, character replacement, wardrobe changes, hairstyle changes, teleportation, instant cutting, duplicated people, inconsistent shadows, sudden weather changes, excessive wind, text, subtitles, logos, watermark, background music, commercial advertisement style. <<<259c3755-e438-49f5-b983-e6673a6ad05c>>> <<<259c3755-e438-49f5-b983-e6673a6ad05c>>>
```

Scene 2: Inside the helicopter cockpit. She wears a green aviation headset with boom mic and a black 4-point harness. A small fuzzy lavalier mic is clipped to her top. She looks at the camera, slightly nervous then smiling, adjusting her seatbelt. Night city lights visible through the open side.

### 受欺凌少女在神社庭院御风反击

> Bullied, shoved, her pages torn apart. The wind had other plans. Created with seedance 2.5 on @Sjolt_ai Prompt: One single continuous 20-second shot, 16:9 lands…

[<img src="https://media.goodcase.ai/cases/28a06e332829.jpg" width="600" alt="受欺凌少女在神社庭院御风反击">](https://goodcase.ai/cases/seedance-2-5-one-single-continuous-20-second-shot-16-9-landscape-1080p-06b62ee1c343?utm_source=awesome-seedance&utm_medium=github&utm_campaign=gallery&utm_content=entry)

**作者:** @Chaemate_ | **来源:** [原帖](https://x.com/Chaemate_/status/2107786193085882582) | **发布:** 2026-10-07 | **热度:** 64

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（53 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
One single continuous 20-second shot, 16:9 landscape, 1080p. NO cuts, NO edits, NO transitions, NO fades, NO title card, NO slow-motion. One unbroken handheld-gimbal camera that moves with the action the entire time. Photoreal supernatural school drama.

STORY
A quiet girl is cornered by four classmates in an old shrine courtyard behind her school. They tear out the pressed-flower pages she has been collecting in her journal and shove her down. Her hurt turns to resolve, and the wind itself answers her. She defeats the four one at a time with acrobatic moves that create visible gusts, calls her flying pages back out of the air, and walks away with them.

REFERENCES
@image1 = the heroine. One girl shown in two views (full body and face close-up); both views are the SAME person. Keep her exact face, dark teal-black layered shoulder-length hair with side-swept bangs, silver hairclip, brown eyes, small beauty mark, slim build.
@image2 = four antagonists, mapped left to right: A = tall, long straight black hair, center part (the leader); B = chestnut bob with blunt bangs; C = high black ponytail with white scrunchie; D = wavy dark-brown hair past the shoulders. Keep each face and hairstyle independent and consistent; never swap identities.
All five wear the reference uniform exactly: navy double-button blazer with white lapel trim, white shirt, red ribbon tie, plaid pleated knee-length skirt, black tights, brown loafers. No other clothing. Do NOT show the white studio backgrounds, lineup poses or split-screen layouts from the references. All five people are the same real-world scale.

NO DISAPPEARING (CRITICAL)
Exactly five real people exist in this scene from the first frame to the last frame: the heroine, A, B, C and D. NOBODY vanishes, fades, dissolves, turns into leaves or dust, teleports, gets blown out of the scene, or is swallowed by the wind. Wind never removes a person from the shot. When the camera moves away from a character and returns, she is still exactly where she was left. Every defeated girl stays physically on the ground in her defeated position, visible, solid and breathing, until the very last frame. Nobody walks or runs out of the courtyard. The count of people stays at five at all times.

SETTING
An old shrine courtyard behind a high school, late-afternoon golden light, soft overcast sky. Gravel and stone-slab ground, two moss-covered stone lanterns, a small wooden shrine gate (torii) in the background, a large ginkgo tree dropping yellow leaves. A low wooden bench on the left. Everything is flat and open, no stairs, no drops, no water. The wind is NOT a storm: it is a controlled, localized swirl of a limited number of yellow ginkgo leaves, light gravel dust and moving hair. The leaves stay thin and sparse, never form a thick cloud or wall, and never cover a person's body or face. Wind is transparent: no glowing beams, no neon, no smoke, no animal shapes, no tornado.

PROPS
One small hand-bound journal with a green cloth cover. Exactly five thin washi-paper pages, each holding a pressed flower (a pink cosmos, a blue hydrangea, a red maple leaf, a tiny forget-me-not, a white camellia), are torn out together. The journal is dropped near the heroine's feet and stays there until she picks it up. The five pages float in the air at the left side of the courtyard during the fight and never act as weapons. Two school bags rest against the shrine wall from the start and are never touched.

CAMERA, ONE CONTINUOUS MOVE
The camera starts in a tight handheld medium shot on the heroine's face, drifts around her, then follows each action in a smooth flowing path at eye level and side three-quarter angles, mostly medium to medium-full so every acrobatic move is fully visible. It never cuts. It sweeps from character to character with whip-pan-style swings that settle on the next target. After each defeat, the camera glides briefly across the fallen girl so the audience sees she is still there. At the climax the camera does one 100-degree orbit around the heroine at normal speed. In the final seconds the camera slowly pulls back to a medium-wide view that shows all five people.

PERFORMANCE TIMELINE (all in one take)

0–4s: Camera starts close on the heroine, holding her green journal against her chest. A (long black hair) snatches it, holds it out of reach and says in a mocking voice, in English: "This is trash." She tears five pressed-flower pages out in one handful and flings them into the air while the others laugh. Camera pans with the pages fluttering, then returns.

4–7s: She turns to chase the pages. B (chestnut bob) steps in and shoves her; she stumbles, drops to one knee and one palm, the journal falls near her. A few loose ginkgo leaves skitter across the gravel. Camera pushes in on her wet eyes and tight jaw. Laughter fades. Hold the hurt for a beat. A, B, C and D are all visible standing nearby.

7–9s: She says quietly and firmly, in English: "Stop it already." Her hair lifts, a few leaves begin to circle, and the five pages stop and hover on the left. The four step back, uneasy, all clearly visible. B moves in first.

9–12s: DEFEAT 1, B. The heroine pushes off her palm into a single handspring into a side cartwheel. Her spinning legs pull the air into one narrow gust that travels sideways across the gravel into B's chest. B is thrown off balance, slides, and ends seated against the left stone lantern base. She stays there, visible, for the rest of the video.

12–16s: DEFEAT 2, C. The camera glances at B still seated, then whips to C, who charges from the right. The heroine drops into a low crouch and slides on the gravel into one long circular leg sweep. The sweep sends a flat current of leaves and dust along the ground into C's ankles. C's feet slip and she falls forward onto her hands and knees at the right edge. She stays there, visible.

16–20s: DEFEAT 3, D. D rushes in with both hands out. The heroine springs up, performs one standing back handspring into a backflip. As she rotates, her heels drag a wide, flat wall of air in front of her that hits D at torso height. D is pushed back two steps and sits down hard on the wooden bench. She stays seated, visible. Nobody collides with anyone else.

DEFEAT POSITIONS (never reset)
B: seated at the left stone lantern. C: hands and knees at the right edge. D: seated on the bench. A: seated alone near the torii gate. All four stay conscious, remain in the courtyard, never rejoin the fight and are never hit by another person's attack.

RULES FOR THE POWERS
Every technique follows: acrobatic movement, wind forms, wind travels, wind makes contact, reaction. The heroine's feet never touch anyone. Each gust fades after its own hit. Only the targeted person is affected; the others may flinch. No blades of air, no blood, no injuries, no one hits their head. One execution per move: no repeated flips, no extra spins.

AUDIO
Natural courtyard ambience, distant cicadas, rustling leaves, gravel scrapes, shoes, paper tearing, mocking laughter, breathing, cloth movement, wind whooshes, bodies landing on gravel. Only TWO spoken lines in the whole video, both in clear natural English with accurate lip sync: A says "This is trash." and the heroine says "Stop it already." No other dialogue. No music, no narrator, no subtitles, no on-screen text.

LOOK
Natural daylight color with warm golden tones, fine film grain, soft halation, real skin texture, no plastic faces, no HDR glow, no vignette, no letterboxing. Side-on, athletic stunt framing; clothing moves naturally with the acrobatics, never framed as spectacle.

AVOID
Anyone disappearing, fading, dissolving, teleporting or leaving the courtyard; a storm, tornado or thick leaf cloud; cuts, split-screens, studio backgrounds, duplicated heroine, extra characters, group takedowns, changed uniforms, mint or green hair on anyone, sliding planted feet, extra limbs, extra pages (exactly five), extra dialogue, music, subtitles, logos.

END
Exactly 20.0 seconds in one unbroken take. The heroine stands holding her journal with all five pressed-flower pages recovered. All four defeated girls are visibly present in their separate positions. Two bags remain by the shrine wall.
```

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（103 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic personal home-video showing an ordinary Sunday morning in the life of a young Korean woman. No reference image.

MAIN SUBJECT

Young Korean woman in her early 20s, natural and approachable look, realistic skin texture with visible pores, bare-faced or barely-there makeup, warm and slightly playful expression.

Shoulder-length wavy black hair left down, side-swept bangs, small mole near her left eye.

Wearing an oversized pastel-yellow hoodie, loose gray jogger pants, fuzzy slide sandals, and a small crossbody pouch bag.

Maintain the same face, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and appearance throughout the entire video.

SETTING

A small countryside town on a bright, breezy Sunday morning.

A traditional outdoor market with striped awnings, crates of vegetables, a fishmonger's stall, an old bus stop with a rusted bench, a narrow road running alongside rice paddies, a local bakery with a hand-painted sign, a grandmother's front yard with drying red peppers on a mat, chickens wandering nearby, and mountains faintly visible in the background. Everything sun-bleached, dusty, and authentically rural.

CAMERA / VISUAL AESTHETIC

Raw personal footage casually recorded by a friend, strong handheld vlog feel throughout.

Constant natural hand shake and micro-jitters, footsteps visibly bouncing the frame, imperfect and often off-center framing, occasional partial crops of her face or shoulder, autofocus visibly hunting between her and the background, exposure flaring when the camera swings toward the sun, mild motion blur on quick turns, soft image detail, subtle digital noise, slightly warm and faded color grading like an old phone camera.

The camera operator walks and jogs to keep up with her, breathing audible at times, camera dipping and swaying with each step.

No stabilization, no gimbal smoothness, no cinematic camera movements, no dramatic lighting, no slow motion, no polished commercial appearance.

The footage should feel like a real, unplanned phone recording — accidentally beautiful rather than deliberately cinematic.

---

00:00–00:05 — MARKET ENTRANCE

She weaves through the outdoor market stalls, the camera bouncing behind her as it struggles to keep pace.

She stops briefly to pick up a small bag of cherry tomatoes, holding them up toward the camera with a grin.

The frame jolts as the operator steps over a crate in the path.

**00:05–00:10 — FISH STALL DETOUR**

She wanders to the fishmonger's stall, wrinkling her nose playfully at the smell and laughing.

The camera whip-pans slightly too fast trying to follow her hand gesture, briefly blurring before catching focus again.

She waves the camera away, still laughing: *"Ugh, don't film that, film me!"*

**00:10–00:15 — BUS STOP WAIT**

She jogs to catch the local bus stop bench just as an old bus pulls away without her, the camera shaking hard from the sudden running motion.

She throws her hands up in mock frustration, plops down on the rusted bench, and sighs dramatically, laughing at herself.

The frame settles slightly off-center as she rests her chin on her palm.

**00:15–00:20 — RICE PADDY ROAD**

She walks along the narrow road beside the rice paddies, wind blowing loose strands across her face.

The camera drifts low near her feet as she kicks a small pebble, then swings back up to her face, slightly overexposed from the open sky.

She spins around once with her arms out, the frame swaying and overshooting past her before correcting.

**00:20–00:25 — GRANDMOTHER'S YARD**

She ducks into a yard where red peppers dry on a mat, crouching to gently shoo away a curious chicken pecking near her sandals.

The camera jerks back suddenly as the chicken flaps its wings, both of them startled and laughing.

She picks up a dried pepper, sniffs it, makes a face, and tosses it back down.

**00:25–00:30 — FINAL MOMENT**

She grabs a warm bag of fresh bread from the nearby bakery counter, takes a big bite, and turns straight to the camera mid-chew.

The frame shakes as she leans in too close, filling half the screen with her grinning face.

She says, muffled and amused:

*"Okay, stop filming me eating!"*

She swats the camera lens with her hand, and the footage cuts abruptly to black, mid-laugh.

---

**AUDIO**

Natural location sound only:

Market chatter, footsteps crunching on gravel, a bus engine pulling away, wind, chickens clucking, rustling plastic bags, distant rooster crow, bread bag crinkling, the operator's breathing and quiet laughter, and audible handheld camera noise.

No music. No narration. No artificial sound effects.

Only the spoken lines: *"Ugh, don't film that, film me!"* and *"Okay, stop filming me eating!"*

**FINAL FEEL**

The entire video should feel like a chaotic, cheerful, completely unplanned Sunday morning caught on a friend's phone — bright, breezy, playful, and warmly nostalgic.

No dramatic events. No exaggerated acting. No model poses. No cinematic hero shots.

The realism should come from constant handheld imperfection, sun glare, dust, uneven pacing, real laughter, and moments that feel genuinely caught rather than staged.
```

Seedance 2.5 on @ElevenLabs @ElevenCreative #ElevenLabsAmbassador 

Prompt

Ultra-realistic Korean K-Drama action thriller featuring Hollywood-level stunt choreography, brutal-looking close-range combat, explosive counterattacks, intense emotional expressions, realistic body physics, powerful impacts, dynamic handheld cinematography, rapid tracking shots, dramatic whip pans, and 120 FPS slow-motion impact inserts.
Professional cinematic lighting, realistic sound design, grounded martial arts choreography, natural human movement, and physically believable collisions.

Every fight sequence must feel intense, aggressive, unpredictable, and visually spectacular without supernatural abilities or cartoon physics.

CHARACTERS

FEMALE LEAD — @[Image 1]\(image_1)

A beautiful 23-year-old Korean college student. Maintain her exact facial identity, hairstyle, clothing, body proportions, and overall appearance from the reference image throughout the entire video.

She initially appears quiet, innocent, and vulnerable, but possesses exceptional martial arts skills, lightning-fast reflexes, extraordinary precision, and controlled aggression.

MAIN BULLY — @[Image 2]\(image_2)

An arrogant 24-year-old Korean male college student with a tall, muscular physique, messy platinum-blonde hair, a black leather jacket, white shirt, dark jeans, and black boots.

Extremely aggressive personality, cruel facial expressions, intimidating body language, and explosive anger.

BULLY 2 — @[Image 3]\(image_3)

A tall, athletic Korean male student with short black hair, wearing a grey school blazer and black trousers. Aggressive, confident, and physically intimidating.

BULLY 3 — @[Image 4]\(image_4)

A muscular Korean male student with brown hair, wearing a black varsity jacket and dark pants. Violent, impulsive, and aggressive.

SCENE 1 (0–4 SEC) — THE PUBLIC HUMILIATION

A long corridor inside a modern Korean college. Metallic blue lockers line both sides of the hallway. Students walk through the corridor under bright overhead lighting.

The female lead @[Image 1]\(image_1) stands beside her open locker, quietly taking out her books.

Suddenly, the platinum-blonde bully @[Image 2]\(image_2) aggressively approaches her.

BOOOOM!

He violently slams her locker door shut with his hand.

The girl instinctively steps backward, visibly startled.

Without warning, the bully snatches the books from her hands and aggressively throws them into the air.

Books and notebooks scatter across the polished corridor floor.

The bully shouts angrily in English:

"HEY! PICK THEM UP!"

His two friends @[Image 3]\(image_3) and @[Image 4]\(image_4) burst into mocking laughter.

The camera rapidly pushes toward the girl's face, capturing her shocked expression in an extreme close-up.

Camera: Aggressive handheld movement, fast push-in, dramatic facial close-up.

SCENE 2 (4–8 SEC) — THE FIRST ATTACK

The girl quietly kneels to collect her scattered books.

Suddenly, the main bully raises his right leg and launches a powerful kick toward her shoulder.

WHOOOOSH!

With lightning-fast reflexes, the girl instantly raises her left forearm and blocks the incoming kick.

THUUUD!

A brief 120 FPS slow-motion insert captures the precise moment of impact.

The bully's expression immediately changes from arrogance to shock.

Before he can recover, the girl explosively rises to her feet.

She controls his extended leg, pivots sharply, and executes a rapid rotational sweep that destroys his balance.

BAAAAM!

The bully crashes sideways onto the floor beside the lockers.

The metallic locker doors shake violently from the nearby collision.

The camera follows his fall with a fast downward tracking movement.

Camera: Extreme close-up, slow-motion kick block, explosive tracking shot.

SCENE 3 (8–13 SEC) — THE LOCKER DOMINO

Bully 2 angrily screams:

"YOU'RE DEAD!"

He charges directly toward the girl at full speed and throws a powerful right-hand punch.

The girl instantly moves her head sideways, narrowly avoiding the strike.

Without hesitation, she counters with a rapid body punch toward his ribs.

THUD!

She immediately follows with a powerful palm strike to his shoulder.

THUD!

The bully stumbles backward.

The girl plants her supporting foot, rotates her hips, and delivers a lightning-fast spinning back kick.

BOOOOOOM!

The kick drives him backward into the metallic blue lockers.

Three adjacent locker doors swing open from the vibration and collide against one another in rapid succession.

CLANG! CLANG! CLANG!

The metallic impacts create a spectacular domino-like chain reaction.

The camera captures the entire collision in a low-angle lateral tracking shot.

Camera: Rapid sideways tracking, low-angle impact framing, realistic metallic vibration.

SCENE 4 (13–19 SEC) — TWO AGAINST ONE

The main bully struggles back to his feet.

At the same moment, Bully 3 charges forward.

Both attackers rush toward the girl simultaneously.

The main bully throws a violent left hook.

The girl instantly ducks beneath the punch.

Bully 3 launches a powerful front kick.

She pivots sharply to the side.

The kick misses its intended target, causing Bully 3 to collide awkwardly with his own friend.

The girl immediately launches an explosive counterattack.

Rapid combat choreography:

A lightning-fast defensive block redirects an incoming strike.

A powerful right palm strike forces the first attacker backward.

A rapid spinning kick interrupts the second attacker's forward momentum.

A low sweeping takedown knocks one bully off balance.
A close-range shoulder throw sends the remaining attacker crashing onto the floor.
BAM! THUD! CRASH!
One bully collapses beside the lockers.
The other slides across the polished corridor floor.

The camera continuously circles the female lead, following every block, pivot, strike, and throw without unnecessary pauses.

All movements must connect naturally, with believable momentum, realistic footwork, and clear cause-and-effect choreography.

Camera: Continuous dynamic orbit, aggressive handheld tracking, fast impact framing.

SCENE 5 (19–25 SEC) — THE FINAL EXPLOSIVE COMBO

The platinum-blonde main bully pushes himself off the floor, trembling with anger.

His face twists into a furious expression.

He screams:

"I'LL KILL YOU!"

He charges toward the girl at full speed and launches a powerful straight punch.

At the very last moment, she sharply dodges sideways.

The bully's fist slams directly into a metallic locker door.

CLAAAAANG!

The locker door bends inward slightly from the impact.

The girl instantly controls his extended wrist.

Using his forward momentum, she executes a powerful rotational throw.

BOOOOOOM!

The bully crashes onto the corridor floor.

He desperately attempts to stand and throws another punch.

The girl blocks it with exceptional precision.

She immediately delivers two rapid, controlled body strikes.

THUD! THUD!

Without giving him time to recover, she rotates into a spectacular spinning hook kick, making controlled contact near his shoulder.

WHOOOOOSH — THUUUUD!

The bully loses his balance completely and collapses onto the floor.

A brief 120 FPS slow-motion insert emphasizes the final kick, followed immediately by a return to real-time speed.

The camera drops into a dramatic low-angle shot, revealing the girl standing in a powerful martial arts stance.

Her breathing is controlled.

Her expression is cold and fearless.

Camera: Explosive whip pans, close-range impacts, brief slow-motion final kick, dramatic low-angle hero shot.

SCENE 6 (25–30 SEC) — THE WRONG STUDENT

All three bullies sit defeated on the corridor floor, shocked and visibly frightened.

The surrounding students stand completely silent.

The main bully looks up at the girl with fear in his eyes.

She calmly bends down and collects her scattered books.

She straightens her clothes and slowly approaches him.

The camera moves into an extreme close-up of her face.

Maintaining a cold, confident expression, she looks directly into his eyes and says in English:

"You picked the wrong girl."

The bully instinctively moves backward in fear.

Without another word, she turns around and calmly walks down the corridor, carrying her books.

The camera slowly pulls backward.

Final cinematic shot:

The three defeated bullies remain on the floor, surrounded by rattling metallic lockers, scattered notebooks, and stunned students.

The female lead disappears into the distance without looking back.

CUT TO BLACK.

SOUND DESIGN

Highly realistic, aggressive cinematic sound effects throughout the entire sequence.

Explosive metallic locker slams and vibrations.

Rapid footsteps and realistic shoe friction.

Sharp punch and kick whooshes.

Heavy, grounded body impacts.
Clothing movement and fabric friction.
Realistic breathing and exertion.
Students gasping and reacting.
Loud metallic crashes during the locker domino sequence.
Dramatic bass drops synchronized with major impacts.
Rising tension effects during the final confrontation.
BACKGROUND MUSIC: Dark Korean action-thriller soundtrack featuring aggressive percussion, deep cinematic bass, escalating tension, rapid rhythmic beats, and explosive impact accents.
Reduce the music briefly during the final dialogue to emphasize the girl's voice.
CAMERA DIRECTION

Time

Camera movement

0–4s
Handheld medium shots, aggressive push-in, extreme facial close-up
4–8s
Close-range tracking, 120 FPS slow-motion kick block
8–13s
Rapid lateral tracking, low-angle locker collision
13–19s
Continuous dynamic camera orbit during the two-on-one fight
19–25s
Fast whip pans, close-range impact shots, brief slow-motion final kick
25–30s
Dramatic facial close-up, slow cinematic dolly-out
STRICT GENERATION RULES
1. Character identity lock: Maintain the exact facial identities, hairstyles, outfits, and body proportions of all four reference characters throughout the entire 30 seconds.
2. Realistic martial arts: Every punch, kick, block, sweep, and throw must feature physically believable movement, grounded footwork, natural momentum, and realistic impact reactions.
3. Maximum fighting intensity: The female lead must fight with exceptional speed, precision, agility, and controlled aggression. Avoid unnecessary pauses, repetitive attacks, or slow choreography.
4. Clear action continuity: Maintain consistent character positions and spatial relationships throughout the fight. Every movement must connect logically to the next.

5. Realistic collisions: Locker impacts, falling bodies, and metallic vibrations must follow believable physics. No flying bodies or exaggerated supernatural force.

6. Precise combat timing: Every strike must have clear physical contact or a realistic near miss. Avoid punches passing through bodies or disconnected reactions.

7. Camera consistency: Use professional cinematic camera movements that emphasize action intensity while keeping the choreography readable.

8. Dialogue accuracy: All spoken dialogue must be in English, with accurate lip synchronization and natural Korean-accented English delivery.

9. Visual consistency: Maintain the same Korean college corridor, metallic blue lockers, lighting conditions, and background environment throughout the video.

10. No generation errors: No character swapping, facial distortion, extra limbs, disappearing objects, teleportation, unnatural body movement, cartoon physics, excessive blood, or gore.

FINAL PRIORITY: Maximum action intensity, explosive close-range martial arts, ultra-realistic Korean revenge-thriller cinematography, seamless fight choreography, realistic physical impacts, nonstop momentum, satisfying revenge, and a powerful final dialogue:

"You picked the wrong girl."

A terrifying Halloween night unfolds on a rain-soaked wooden porch, where a real adult man discovers that his carved jack-o'-lantern is behaving strangely. 12–21 seconds: The pumpkin's light abruptly goes out. Complete silence. 21–27 seconds: The man slowly reaches toward the pumpkin again. Before he can touch it, the jack-o'-lantern rotates several inches toward him on its own. Its carved smile remains perfectly still. Frozen with disbelief, he slowly backs toward the door.

27–30 seconds: Extreme close-up of the pumpkin. Its internal light suddenly switches back on. A tiny, fresh handprint gradually appears in condensation across its outer surface. Before the viewer can understand what has happened, the screen cuts instantly to black.

Sound design includes rainfall, distant wind, wooden footsteps, soft breathing, subtle fabric movement, a low suspenseful musical rise, and sudden silence before the final reveal.

Negative prompt: No animation, cartoon visuals, CGI-looking characters, artificial environments, changing faces, inconsistent clothing, distorted anatomy, unrealistic pumpkin movement, exaggerated jump scares, gore, dialogue, narration, subtitles, text, logos, watermarks, black bars, or abrupt transitions before the final cut.

```
TITLE: "TRY THIS ROADSTER". A 28-second high-budget live-action car commercial, filmed for real on location by a professional crew with camera cars, a drone, a runway crane and an air-to-air camera jet. Shot on 35mm film: warm natural light, soft halation, fine grain, real motion blur, smooth stabilised moves with subtle handheld life. Every shot is at real-time speed with no slow motion, cut fast and punchy. The effects are flawless and invisible. Nothing looks digital or animated.

ASSETS:
- Roadster: the car, with the same shape, silver-white colour, scale and RDSTR plate in every shot.
- Character: the woman, with the same face, hair and outfit throughout.
- Racer: the man, an original racing driver in a plain white-and-silver race suit with no logos.
- Interior: the cabin, left-hand drive, with her always seated on the left behind the yoke.
- FlightFX: whenever the car hovers or flies, exactly four thin, semi-transparent white cold-gas vapour jets blow straight down from beneath it. They are soft and misty, with no glow and no flame.
- Courtyard, Desert and Runway: the locations. Each scene stays strictly in its own location.
Use every image for appearance only, never its background.

PHYSICAL REALISM: the car is always lit by the scene's own sun, casts a real moving shadow, reflects its surroundings, and is a normal 4.5 m sports car in scale. The camera always physically moves with the action.

SCENE 1, COURTYARD [0s-7s], location Courtyard (old stone college building, soft overcast daylight):
[0-1.5s] LOW DETAIL: the camera glides past the car's front wheel and sculpted silver flank, with the two people walking toward it in the background.
[1.5-3.5s] MEDIUM, OVER THE SHOULDER: Racer rests a hand on the car's roof, smiles and says, "Try this Roadster." Character, facing him, lights up and replies, "It looks amazing!"
[3.5-5.5s] MEDIUM: she opens the driver's door, slides into the left seat and runs her hand over the yoke, admiring the cabin.
[5.5-7s] LOW WIDE: the door closes with a solid thunk and the car starts with a soft electric hum.

SCENE 2, DRIVE-AWAY [7s-8s]: LOW TRACKING on a leafy street. The car accelerates past the lens with a rush of air, a light smoky haze curling off the tyres toward camera.

SCENE 3, DESERT CAMELS [8s-12s], location Desert (golden-hour highway, dunes, acacias):
[8-9s] CHASE behind the car: about 12 real camels scattered naturally on and beside the road turn their heads toward it, and one groans.
[9-10s] LOW REAR: the four vapour jets fire downward, smoke and sand billow out from beneath the car, and it lifts off the asphalt.
[10-11s] LOW ANGLE among the herd: a camel's head fills the foreground. Behind it, the car flies overhead and completes one smooth barrel roll around its nose-to-tail axis, with thin vapour trails behind it.
[11-12s] LOW REAR CHASE: the car lands softly on the road in a burst of smoke and dust and drives on.

SCENE 4, STREET SWEEPER [12s-15s], suburban autumn street:
[12-13s] MEDIUM WIDE: a tired street sweeper in an orange high-visibility vest stands beside a huge pile of leaves. The car hovers low beside the kerb, and she smiles at him from her open window.
[13-15s] LOW GROUND: the jets blast the pile into a swirling cloud of orange leaves and vapour. It clears to reveal a spotless sidewalk and the sweeper laughing in amazement.

SCENE 5, APPLE TREE [15s-17s], sunny green orchard:
[15-16s] WIDE TRACKING: the car flies in low and hovers beside a big apple tree heavy with red apples, its jets rustling the grass.
[16-17s] MEDIUM CLOSE-UP at her window: she reaches out, twists and plucks a shiny red apple, the branch springs back, and she smiles at it.

SCENE 6, CABIN [17s-18s]: MEDIUM INTERIOR side profile. She drives with one hand on the yoke and a relaxed, happy smile, warm light streaming through the glass.

SCENE 7, RUNWAY TO THE SKY [18s-28s], location Runway (golden sunset, unbranded white private jet):
[18-19.5s] LOW WIDE: the car rolls on its wheels down the runway beside the jet, both accelerating, the jet's engines shimmering with heat.
[19.5-21s] DETAIL with RACK FOCUS: the car's vapour jets blast down onto the runway, smoke billows out, its tyres lift, and focus racks to the jet's nose rotating up.
[21-22.5s] WIDE: both lift off together and climb toward golden clouds. The car pulls ahead, vapour streaming continuously.
[22.5-24s] CHASE: the car punches through a cloud and bursts out above a glowing sea of clouds.
[24-25.5s] WIDE AIR-TO-AIR: six to eight real white geese glide in on both sides of the car, flying alongside it.
[25.5-26.5s] TIGHT, SHALLOW FOCUS at her window: a goose in the soft foreground. She smiles and waves goodbye.
[26.5-28s] HIGH WIDE: the car tilts its nose down and descends through the clouds toward the golden land below, vapour trailing.

SOUND: real location audio only. Footsteps and dialogue in the courtyard, a door thunk and electric hum, a whoosh as the car passes, camel groans, the deep hiss and whoosh of the thrusters, leaves roaring, the sweeper's laugh, birdsong and an apple snap, a jet-engine roar, rushing wind, goose wing flaps and calls. No music, so it can be added in the edit.

Avoid: slow motion, CGI, an animated look, a plastic look, the car changing shape or size, glowing jets, rocket flames, fire, an evenly front-lit or pasted-in car, missing shadows, a front flip, a backflip, the woman on the right, a round steering wheel, team or sponsor logos, real celebrities, cartoon animals, birds frozen in the air, a white or studio background, text, watermark.

Let's go to shopping and be kind to kids

Seedance 2.5 on @TapNow_AI

Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic early-2000s DV home video of a 20-year-old Japanese woman spending a quiet late-summer afternoon in an old seaside neighborhood in Kamakura, Japan.

**MAIN SUBJECT**

A 20-year-old Japanese woman, naturally beautiful, realistic skin texture, minimal makeup, long dark-brown wavy hair loosely tied with a simple fabric hair tie, wearing a fitted pale-yellow short-sleeve top, loose light-blue wide-leg trousers, white canvas sneakers, and a delicate silver necklace. She leaves a small traditional Japanese rental house carrying a woven shoulder tote bag and walks through a narrow residential lane toward a tiny local bakery near the coast.

**SCENE 1 — LEAVING HOME**

She steps outside, slides the wooden door closed behind her, adjusts her tote bag and walks down a quiet narrow street lined with old Japanese houses, bicycles, potted plants, utility poles and overhead wires.

She looks toward the camera and casually says:

**“I finally have a slow afternoon.”**

The camera follows her from behind with imperfect handheld movement.

**SCENE 2 — LOCAL BAKERY**

She enters a tiny old-fashioned neighborhood bakery with a wooden entrance and glass display case.

She chooses a melon pan, a small red-bean pastry and a bottled ramune. She places everything carefully inside her woven tote bag.

While looking through the small refrigerator, she notices several colorful popsicles.

She picks up two peach-flavored popsicles, smiles and adds them to her bag.

The bakery owner places the items into a small paper package and hands it to her.

She politely says:

**“Thank you, I’ll come again.”**

**SCENE 3 — SEASIDE STREET**

Outside, she walks toward the coast.

She notices two neighborhood children sitting near a low stone wall, watching bicycles pass by.

She takes the two peach popsicles from her bag and gives one to each child.

Each child clearly receives one and happily holds it.

One child says:

**“Really? Thank you!”**

She smiles and replies:

**“Enjoy it before it melts.”**

The children happily begin eating their popsicles.

The popsicles visibly transfer from the bakery refrigerator → woman's tote bag → children's hands. No disappearing or duplicated objects.

**SCENE 4 — BICYCLE**

As she continues walking, she notices one child's bicycle has fallen over beside the sidewalk.

She stops, picks it up and holds the handlebars while the child checks the bicycle.

The child smiles and says:

**“You helped me!”**

She laughs softly and replies:

**“No problem. Ride carefully.”**

The child rides away with the other child.

**SCENE 5 — WATER FOUNTAIN**

She continues toward the seaside and discovers an old public water fountain beside a small park.

She turns the metal tap.

Water begins flowing naturally.

She cups her hands under the water and splashes cool water onto her face.

She laughs naturally, shakes the water from her hands and wipes her face.

She looks refreshed and says:

**“That feels so good.”**

**SCENE 6 — WALKING HOME**

The late-afternoon sunlight falls across the quiet Japanese street.

She walks back toward her house carrying the same woven tote bag.

A bicycle bell rings somewhere in the distance.

She briefly looks directly into the DV camera and says:

**“Best kind of afternoon.”**

She smiles, turns toward her house and walks through the wooden entrance.

The camera remains outside for a moment as the door closes.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

**CAMERA / VISUAL STYLE**

Raw handheld early-2000s Japanese DV camcorder footage. Authentic consumer camcorder aesthetic, shaky handheld movement, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting, occasional focus breathing, faded slightly washed-out colors, soft digital detail, mild compression artifacts, exposure shifts

prompt

Image1 Main Subject: Young woman, 20, natural everyday appearance, extremely long wavy blonde hair in a loose half-up style with wispy bangs, realistic skin texture, minimal makeup, slightly sleepy but cheerful personality. follow only the proportion of cleavage from this reference image Image1 

Wearing a soft mustard-yellow cardigan over a white cotton blouse with a deep plunging scoop neckline that that accentuates her large cleavage, dark navy pleated midi skirt, white ankle socks, brown loafers, and a small canvas shoulder bag. Maintain consistent identity, clothing, hairstyle, and appearance throughout.

Location: Quiet traditional neighborhood in Gyeongju on a cool autumn morning. Low tiled-roof houses, stone walls, narrow lanes, small gardens, bicycles, old utility poles, fallen leaves, and a tiny family-owned bakery with a fogged glass window and handwritten Korean signs. No tourists or busy traffic.

Visual Style: Ultra-realistic documentary realism. Genuine candid behavior, subtle expressions, natural movement, authentic Korean small-town atmosphere.

Camera Style: Early-2000s consumer MiniDV camcorder. Heavy handheld shake, imperfect framing, autofocus hunting between bakery windows and her face, exposure pumping between indoor and outdoor light, faded colors, soft contrast, slight motion blur, DV compression artifacts, mild sensor noise, no stabilization.

00:00–00:03
She walks down the quiet lane carrying her shoulder bag, stepping through fallen leaves. She notices the small bakery and slows down.

00:03–00:06 — Bakery
Inside, she looks through the glass display and chooses a freshly baked red-bean bun. Warm bakery light reflects on the glass while autofocus struggles to find her face.

The elderly bakery owner smiles and says softly:
English subtitle: “Fresh out of the oven.” (방금 구운 거예요.)

She gives a small sleepy smile and replies:
English subtitle: “Thank you.” (감사합니다.)

00:06–00:09
She steps outside, takes a small bite, and smiles while warm steam escapes from the fresh bread. A bicycle passes behind her.

She quietly says to herself:
English subtitle: “It’s warm.” (따뜻하다.)

00:09–00:12
She continues toward a nearby stone wall, checking the time on her small wristwatch as morning sunlight reaches the street.

She looks toward the camera and murmurs:
English subtitle: “I’m going to be late.” (늦겠다.)

00:12–00:15
She notices the camera, laughs softly, gives a quick little wave, and walks away toward the old neighborhood. The operator accidentally points at the pavement before the recording continues.

She laughs and says:
English subtitle: “Bye.” (안녕.)

00:15–00:18 — Small neighborhood moment
After she walks away, the camera follows her loosely. She passes an elderly local resident sweeping fallen leaves near a stone wall. They exchange a brief polite nod.

The elderly resident says:
English subtitle: “Good morning.” (좋은 아침이에요.)

She responds naturally:
English subtitle: “Good morning.” (안녕하세요.)

The camera lags behind slightly.

00:18–00:21 — Autumn detail
She stops beside a tiny garden and notices a few red autumn leaves caught on a bicycle basket. She gently picks one up, looks at it for a second, then puts it back.

She quietly says:
English subtitle: “Pretty.” (예쁘다.)

The camcorder briefly loses focus.

00:21–00:24 — Old neighborhood atmosphere
She continues walking past traditional houses. A small dog appears behind a low gate and watches her. She smiles and gives it a tiny wave.

She softly says to the dog:
English subtitle: “Hi.” (안녕.)

The dog remains behind the gate.

00:24–00:27 — Bakery callback
She suddenly realizes she is still holding the paper bag. She looks inside, takes another small bite of the red-bean bun, and keeps walking.

She smiles and says:
English subtitle: “So good.” (맛있다.)

The bakery door chime can faintly be heard in the distance.

00:27–00:30 — Imperfect ending
She reaches the end of the narrow lane and turns back toward the camera for one last smile. She raises her hand in a casual goodbye.

She says softly:
English subtitle: “See you.” (또 봐요.)

The operator tries to zoom in, but the image becomes soft and shaky. The camera accidentally tilts toward the autumn sky and tiled rooftops, then down toward the pavement. Abrupt MiniDV cut to black.

Audio: Natural ambience only–morning birds, footsteps on leaves, bicycle bell, distant scooter, bakery door chime, quiet conversation, paper bag rustling, soft wind, and the girl's natural Korean dialogue. Dialogue should sound spontaneous, quiet, and conversational, never like scripted acting. No music. No narration.

Subtitle Style: Burned-in English subtitles for every spoken Korean line, with the original Korean dialogue immediately after the English subtitle in parentheses. Keep subtitles small, simple, white, and slightly imperfect like early consumer-video captions. No modern stylized typography, no animated captions, no text overlays other than the dialogue subtitles.

Goal: A forgotten MiniDV recording from 2004, capturing an ordinary autumn morning in historic Gyeongju–quiet, nostalgic, imperfect, intimate, and completely believable.

<summary><b>完整 prompt（141 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
KAZEMIRU: THE JADE-WIND BLADE: ONE-TAKE CASTLE ASSAULT
Duration: 30 seconds | Aspect Ratio: 16:9 | No music

ONE-TAKE LOCK:
This is ONE SINGLE UNBROKEN CONTINUOUS SHOT lasting the full 30 seconds.
ZERO cuts. ZERO dissolves. ZERO fades. ZERO match-cuts. ZERO time jumps.
ZERO scene resets. The camera never leaves the action and every location
change happens physically inside the shot: the camera passes through torn
doors, over railings and up the roof with Kazemiru. Time runs in real time.

STYLE:
Ultra-photorealistic live-action Japanese dark-action cinema with restrained
subtle VFX. Impossible anime-inspired dual-sword choreography performed by
real human bodies. Large-format cinema look, natural daylight, muted earthy
color palette, soft realistic shadows, no oversaturated colors.

@Image1 is KAZEMIRU. Preserve her exact face, silver-white ponytail, torn
ivory kimono, charcoal split hakama, wind-swirl forearm tattoo and gold hoop
earrings in every frame.
@Image2 shows the five elite guards. @Image3 shows Captain Tetsugan.

POSITIVE LOCKS (true for all 30 seconds):
- Exactly ONE Kazemiru with exactly TWO real steel katanas, one in each
  hand, both always visible and independently controlled.
- Her face always matches @Image1.
- Every enemy comes from @Image2 or @Image3 and stays visually consistent.
- Her body physically crosses every distance, with no teleportation.
- It is always daytime with bright natural light.

PERSONALITY:
Cold, calm, battle-hungry. Minimal expression, steady breathing, focused
eyes. Her hunger shows through relentless speed and escalating ferocity,
never through grinning or shouting.

JADE-WIND EFFECT:
Every fast movement leaves a thin, soft jade-green wind trace that follows
her exact physical path and fades almost instantly: sword arcs leave fine
blade trails, spins bend the air, footsteps kick tiny wakes, supersonic
bursts add a brief compressed-air ripple. Always thin, subtle and realistic.
No aura, no smoke, no glowing body, no effects moving on their own.

CAMERA (one continuous handheld-style pursuit):
Constant dutch angle of 25–35°. Wide field of view around 75° in the
interior and courtyard, tightening to about 50° for the rooftop duel.
Kazemiru moves first and the camera follows 0.1–0.3 seconds late: whip-pan,
overshoot, correct, reacquire at the moment of contact. The camera flows
through rear over-the-shoulder, low-angle pursuit, side tracking,
ground-level chase and front three-quarter retreat, always in one connected
movement. Never static, never perfectly smooth.

══ 0:00–0:10: THE GRAND AUDIENCE HALL ══
Setting: bright daylight through large shoji panels, gold folding screens,
tall dark timber pillars, tatami sections, raised platform, armor displays,
low lacquer tables, silk curtains.

0:00–0:03: Rear over-the-shoulder, five guards closing in. Her fingers tighten
on both hilts. BURST: a thin jade streak snaps across the hall and the silk
curtains whip. The camera loses her, whip-pans, and finds her inside
Guard 1's reach. Left blade parries, right blade slashes diagonally, a pivot,
a reverse cut. He drops.

0:03–0:06: Guards 2 and 3 converge. She ducks under a swing, plants a foot on
a pillar and pushes off, dust bursting from the wood. Mid-air her left
sword deflects Guard 2 and her right sword cuts down. She lands on the edge
of a lacquer table, which slides back, and rebounds straight toward Guard 3.

0:06–0:10: Aerial right slash, left reverse slash, dual cross-slash with two
separate jade trails. She lands low and bursts across the floor. Guard 4's
spear thrusts: she runs two steps up a wall, flips off, and cuts him down
through a folding screen. Guard 5 charges: she catches his blade with one
sword, vaults the railing above him, and finishes with a cross-slash as she
lands behind him.

══ 0:10–0:20: THE PALACE COURTYARD ══
Transition inside the same shot: she does not pose. Both swords still in
hand, she drives straight through the paper doors in one burst. The torn
paper flutters past the lens as the camera follows her through the doorway
onto the wooden veranda and out into a stone courtyard with lanterns, a koi
pond, a red lacquer arched bridge and pine trees. Eight new guards: swordsmen
below and archers on the roofs.

0:10–0:13: A volley of arrows. She cuts two out of the air with a spinning
double slash and a third grazes her sleeve. She sprints along the veranda,
pivoting off each post, a jade ripple following her feet.

0:13–0:16: She kicks off a stone lantern, which tips over, and leaps onto the
bridge railing. Three swordsmen charge. She runs along the rail, slashes the
first, flips over the second, and lands in the koi pond with a huge splash.
In the water she parries two blades and cuts both guards. Droplets scatter
across the lens.

0:16–0:20: She bursts out of the pond and sprints up a pine trunk in three
steps. The camera tilts up with her. She launches onto the roof among the
archers: left sword disarms, right sword strikes, roof tiles scatter. Two
archers fall.

══ 0:20–0:30: THE ROOFTOP DUEL ══
Transition inside the same shot: she keeps climbing over the roof ridge and
the camera rises with her, tightening to a 50° field of view. The tiled
castle rooftop opens up with wind in her ponytail and the town far below.
Captain Tetsugan (@Image3) waits with his naginata, calm and unflinching.

0:20–0:23: Tetsugan sweeps the naginata in a wide arc. Kazemiru ducks, slides
down the sloping tiles, and crosses both swords to stop the blade. Sharp
metal sound as his strength pushes her back a step. Her expression stays
cold.

0:23–0:27: Fast exchange: his thrust, her left parry, her right slash that cuts
his armor cord. He spins the pole and sweeps for her feet: she jumps, kicks
off the shaft, and flips over him. Mid-air she cuts both swords across his
back armor. Tiles crack under their feet and a faint jade wake curves around
her path. He staggers, strikes back, and she narrowly dodges as the blade
cuts a roof beam.

0:27–0:30: Supersonic burst along the ridge line, the jade streak slightly
brighter but still thin. She slides under the naginata, and as she rises
both swords slice through the shaft in a clean cross-cut. Tetsugan drops to
one knee, defeated but alive. Kazemiru stands, both swords still in hand,
and the last jade filaments disappear. She turns her cold eyes toward the
courtyard far below, where dozens more guards pour in. The camera slowly
pulls back and up to a wide shot of the whole castle. She lowers her body for
the next burst. THE FIGHT CONTINUES.

AUDIO:
Sword resonance, compressed-air whooshes, metal clashes, footsteps on wood
and tiles, arrows, water splashes, fluttering silk and paper, enemy shouts,
wind over the rooftops. No dialogue, no music.

HUMAN-FIRST LOCK:
Kazemiru is always a real human: natural skin pores, real eyes, individual
hair strands, believable anatomy and cloth physics. Only her choreography is
anime-level impossible.

NEGATIVE:
cuts, edits, dissolves, fades, scene transitions, time jumps, multiple
shots, single-sword fighting, missing or duplicate sword, duplicate
Kazemiru, clones, teleportation, thick aura, smoke, glowing body, neon or
oversaturated colors, detached VFX, random flips, decorative acrobatics,
idle pose, static camera, perfectly smooth tracking, slow combat, floating,
broken anatomy, CGI or plastic skin, anime rendering, blood, gore, subtitles,
logos, watermark.
```

<details>
<summary><b>完整 prompt（108 行，点开展开）</b></summary>

```
Create a 30-second, 1080p ultra-realistic documentary-style personal home video showing an ordinary summer day in the life of a young Korean man. The footage should feel spontaneous, intimate, imperfect, and genuinely observed rather than performed.
MAIN SUBJECT
The same young Korean man in his early 20s throughout the entire video.
Naturally handsome, realistic skin texture, minimal styling, relaxed expression, slightly tired but peaceful eyes.
He has naturally messy medium-length dark hair with a few strands falling over his forehead and very subtle stubble.
He wears a loose washed-black T-shirt, relaxed olive-beige trousers, worn white sneakers, and a simple silver wristwatch.
Keep his face, identity, body proportions, hairstyle, clothing, watch, and overall appearance completely consistent from beginning to end.
LOCATION
A quiet older residential neighborhood in Seoul during a warm summer afternoon.
Narrow concrete alleys, low-rise homes, rooftop terraces, external staircases, potted plants, laundry lines, parked bicycles, utility poles, overhead wires, mature trees, concrete walls, small residential courtyards, and distant city sounds.
The neighborhood should feel authentic and lived-in.
No crowds, tourist attractions, advertisements, recognizable brands, or commercial activity.
CAMERA / VISUAL STYLE
Authentic casual personal-video footage captured with an older consumer digital camera.
Handheld camera operated by a friend walking nearby.
Natural camera shake, imperfect framing, occasional autofocus changes, slight exposure adjustments when moving between sunlight and shade, soft image detail, mild motion blur, subtle digital noise, slightly muted colors, imperfect white balance, and natural compression.
The camera operator occasionally reacts a little late, cuts off part of the subject, or briefly loses focus.
No stabilization, gimbal movement, drone shots, cinematic camera choreography, dramatic lighting, slow motion, modern commercial color grading, or polished cinematography.
The footage should feel like someone simply decided to record his friend during an ordinary day.
---
00:00–00:05 — MORNING ROOFTOP
He sits casually on a small rooftop beside an old plastic chair.
A cold bottled drink rests beside him.
He looks quietly across the neighborhood while the wind moves his hair and T-shirt.
He takes a sip, notices something happening in the distance, and smiles faintly.
He briefly notices the camera and gives a subtle nod before looking away.
The camera takes a moment to find focus on his face.
---
00:05–00:10 — WALKING THROUGH THE ALLEY
He gets up and walks downstairs into the neighborhood.
He walks casually through a narrow concrete alley with his hands in his pockets.
He passes parked bicycles, potted plants, laundry hanging from balconies, and old residential walls.
The camera follows several steps behind him.
He occasionally looks back toward the camera but never deliberately poses.
His footsteps remain naturally synchronized with his movement.
---
00:10–00:14 — SMALL EVERYDAY MOMENT
He notices an old basketball resting near a wall.
He picks it up, casually bounces it twice, then takes a simple shot toward a nearby hoop.
The shot misses.
He laughs quietly, shakes his head, and leaves the ball where he found it.
The camera briefly loses focus during the movement and recovers naturally.
No exaggerated athletic movement.
---
00:14–00:19 — LOCAL SHOP
He walks to a tiny neighborhood shop and buys a cold drink.
He exchanges a few natural words with the shopkeeper but the conversation is not clearly audible.
He steps outside, opens the bottle, takes a drink, and leans casually against the wall.
He watches bicycles and pedestrians passing in the distance.
The camera remains handheld and slightly imperfect.
---
00:19–00:23 — SUMMER RAIN
A sudden summer shower begins.
He looks toward the sky with mild surprise.
Instead of immediately running for shelter, he smiles and slowly walks into the rain.
The rain becomes heavier.
His hair becomes wet and falls naturally across his forehead.
He eventually starts running down the alley, laughing genuinely.
He briefly spins around while running, then continues toward a covered walkway.
His clothes become visibly damp.
Maintain realistic rain interaction, wet fabric, wet hair, reflections, and foot contact with the ground.
---
00:23–00:27 — QUIET MOMENT
He reaches the covered walkway and catches his breath.
Rain falls heavily behind him.
He wipes water from his forehead and looks quietly toward the street.
For a moment, everything becomes still.
He notices the camera again.
He gives a small genuine smile, not a posed expression.
---
00:27–00:30 — WALKING AWAY
The rain becomes lighter.
He walks away down the wet residential lane.
The camera follows from behind.
Reflections shimmer across the concrete.
He turns his head once, gives a tiny wave toward the camera, smiles, and continues walking.
The camera remains pointed toward the empty street for a brief moment.
At approximately 00:29, the recording abruptly cuts to black mid-motion.
No fade-out.
---
PHYSICAL REALISM
Maintain believable real-world physics throughout.
Hands, fingers, feet, clothing, hair, rain, bottle, basketball, and background objects must behave naturally.
No extra fingers, fused hands, duplicated limbs, distorted anatomy, floating objects, teleportation, disappearing objects, or sudden transformations.
The bottle remains a separate physical object and never intersects with his face.
The basketball behaves naturally and remains where it lands.
Parked bicycles and background objects remain stationary unless physically moved.
His feet remain properly connected to the ground while walking and running.
Keep the environment and subject consistent between shots.
---
AUDIO
Natural environmental audio only.
Footsteps on concrete, distant traffic, birds, leaves moving in the wind, bicycles, faint neighborhood conversations, shop sounds, bottle opening, basketball bouncing, rain hitting concrete, water dripping from rooftops, and subtle camera-handling noise.
No music.
No narration.
No soundtrack.
No artificial sound effects.
No spoken dialogue is necessary.
---
FINAL FEEL

Not a commercial.
Not a fashion film.
Not a music video.
Not a professional cinematic production.
The emotional appeal should come from small human moments: sitting alone, wandering through familiar streets, missing a basketball shot, drinking something cold, getting caught in the rain, laughing, and walking home.
Quiet, masculine, youthful, nostalgic, slightly melancholic, warm, spontaneous, and deeply human.
Prioritize natural behavior, consistent identity, believable physics, imperfect handheld framing, authentic environmental details, and the feeling that the camera happened to be there.

SHOT 2 — 00:05–00:10

SHOT 3 — 00:10–00:15

SHOT 4 — 00:15–00:20

SHOT 5 — 00:20–00:25

SHOT 6 — 00:25–00:30

Both skyscrapers are dark, except for one illuminated floor.
Through the glass, BLACK ROSE and the engineer look upward.
A second bridge, far above them, suddenly lights up.
A human silhouette stands inside it.
Cut to black.

ACTION PHYSICS:
Every movement must show contact, momentum, recoil and believable balance loss.
